# 01 · Database design

**Status:** planning baseline for build · **Date:** 2026-11-27 · **Source of truth:** [`system-design-v2.md`](../system-design-v2.md) · **Requirements:** [`features.md`](../../features.md)

This document defines every table the backend needs for all 180 requirements (P0, P1, P2). Tables are created build by build (column **B**); the design is complete now so later builds extend rather than redesign. Diagrams are in [`02_database_erd.md`](02_database_erd.md); migration order is in [`03_database_migration_plan.md`](03_database_migration_plan.md).

Labels: **[Decision]** adopted here; **[Assumption]** planning number to replace with measurement; **[Needs evidence]** unverified external fact.

---

## 1. Conventions

| Topic | Rule |
|---|---|
| Engine | PostgreSQL 17, extensions `pgvector`, `pg_trgm`, `ltree`, `btree_gist`, `pgcrypto` (only for `gen_random_bytes` in tests; ids come from the application). |
| Schemas | One schema per server module (§2). A module reads and writes only its own schema. Cross-module access goes through the owning module's Python interface. |
| Primary keys | UUIDv7 generated by the application (`uuid` type). Time-ordered, so B-tree inserts stay append-mostly. Reference tables with natural keys use them (`roles.code`, `boards.code`). |
| Time | `timestamptz` stored in UTC. Bangladesh dates are stored explicitly as `date` columns named `*_dhaka_date` or `dhaka_date`, computed in the application with `Asia/Dhaka` (UTC+6, no DST). Never derived at query time in hot paths. |
| Money | **Integer poisha** in `bigint` columns named `*_poisha` with `CHECK (… >= 0)` where negatives are meaningless (user decision 2026-11-27). Currency is always BDT (`currency char(3) DEFAULT 'BDT'` only on provider-facing rows). AI cost is `bigint` micro-USD (`cost_micro_usd`). |
| Marks | `numeric(7,2)` (negative marking of 0.25 needs decimals; never float). |
| Enumerations | `text` with a `CHECK (col IN (…))` constraint, not Postgres `ENUM` types. Adding a value is a constraint swap in an expand migration; `ENUM` values cannot be removed. Open-ended taxonomies (blueprint section types, taxonomy levels) are plain `text` validated by reference tables. |
| JSON | `jsonb` only for payloads whose shape is versioned by the application (answer keys, responses, rule sets, provider payloads). Anything filtered or joined on is a column. |
| Names | snake_case; tables plural; FK columns `<entity>_id`; booleans `is_*`/`has_*`; timestamps `*_at`; dates `*_on` or `*_date`. Constraint names: `pk_<table>`, `fk_<table>_<col>`, `uq_<table>_<cols>`, `ck_<table>_<rule>`, `ix_<table>_<cols>` (Alembic naming convention set once in `core/db.py` metadata). |
| Tenancy | `tenant_id uuid NULL` on content, exam and institution rows. `NULL` = platform content. Row-level security policies are added in Build 8 (INST); the column and the filters in every query exist from Build 2. |
| Soft delete | Only where a lifecycle requires it (`users.deleted_at`, `tutor_threads.deleted_at`). Content uses status (`retired`), never deletes. Ledgers and logs are append-only. |
| Audit columns | `created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()`; `updated_at` only on mutable rows, set by the application (no triggers on hot tables). |
| PII | Phone, email and guardian phone stored encrypted (`*_enc bytea`, AES-256-GCM with a per-environment data key wrapped by KMS) plus a keyed lookup hash (`*_hash bytea`, HMAC-SHA256 with a separate key). Never stored in plain text, never logged. |

### 1.1 Cross-schema foreign keys **[Decision]**

| Allowed | Why |
|---|---|
| Any table → `identity.users(id)` | Users are never hard-deleted (deletion anonymises, §5.9), so the FK is stable and cheap. |
| Any table → immutable content and reference rows: `qbank.question_versions`, `qbank.questions`, `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes`, `curriculum.tracks`, `curriculum.syllabus_versions`, `identity.institutions`, `identity.districts` | These rows are never deleted (retired instead). The FK catches bugs at no meaningful cost. |
| **Not allowed:** FKs into another module's high-volume or partitioned tables (`attempt_answers`, `reward_transactions`, `tutor_messages`, `exam_answer_batches`, `outbox`) | Partitioned targets need the partition key in the FK, and these tables must stay splittable. References are plain `uuid` columns validated by the owning module's interface. |
| **Exception [Decision, R-14]:** `practice.attempt_answers` and `rewards.reward_transactions` have **no outgoing FKs** (not to `identity.users`, `qbank.question_versions` or `identity.track_enrolments`) | These are the hottest insert paths, about 1,700–2,500 tx/s at Stage 4, and an exam copy-in writes 20M rows referencing about 100 `question_versions` rows. Each FK insert takes `FOR KEY SHARE` on the parent row, which causes MultiXact and SLRU contention on a few hot parents. Dropping the FKs also keeps the §5.9 option of moving `practice` + `rewards` to their own cluster. References are validated in the application: the answer service reads the version through `qb.get_versions()`, the user and enrolment come from the authenticated actor, and a nightly orphan check (§5.4) reports any dangling id. |

All cross-schema FKs use `ON DELETE RESTRICT`. Within a schema, children of an aggregate use `ON DELETE CASCADE` only where the parent is genuinely deletable (drafts, folders).

### 1.2 Partitioning rules

PostgreSQL requires every unique constraint on a partitioned table to include the partition key. Each partitioned table below is designed so that its business-uniqueness rule naturally contains that key.

| Table | Method | Key | Partitions | Why |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `practice.attempt_answers` | HASH | `user_id` | **64** | Largest table. Uniqueness is `(user_id, id)` (C4). Per-user queries (notebook recompute, dashboards) hit one partition. Kept forever, so no time-based dropping is needed. At Stage 4 (about 30M rows/day, about 11B/year) 64 partitions keep each partition near 170M rows/year, so per-partition vacuum and index builds stay manageable. The count cannot be changed cheaply later, which is why it is set high now (R-13). |
| `rewards.reward_transactions` | HASH | `user_id` | **64** | Ledger is kept forever; idempotency key is per user. Same sizing reason as `attempt_answers`. |
| `exam.exam_answer_batches`, `exam.exam_answers_latest` | RANGE | `exam_starts_on` (date, copied from the exam) | monthly | Deterministic per attempt, so `(attempt_id, seq, exam_starts_on)` is equivalent to `(attempt_id, seq)`. Old months are archived to S3 and dropped (§5.8). |
| `platform.outbox` | RANGE | `recorded_on` | daily | High churn; dropped 7 days after relay. |
| `platform.processed_events` | RANGE | `event_recorded_on` (from the envelope, deterministic) | daily | Dedupe window 14 days, far beyond the 8-try retry span. |
| `ai.tutor_messages`, `ai.tutor_citations`, `ai.ai_usage` | RANGE | `created_on` | monthly | 12-month thread retention (v2 §4.6); usage kept 24 months for cost audit. |
| `notify.notifications`, `notify.deliveries`, `notify.sms_messages` | RANGE | `created_on` | monthly | 6-month inbox retention; SMS log 24 months (cost audit). |
| `ops.audit_log` | RANGE | `occurred_on` | monthly | Append-only; 3-year retention **[Assumption, D4]**. |
| `exam.integrity_events`, `community.group_messages`, `ops.campaign_sends` | RANGE | `created_on` | monthly | Later-build high-volume logs. |

Partitions are created 3 periods ahead and dropped on schedule by the `platform.partition_maintenance` job (Build 1), driven by a declarative list in code. No `pg_partman` dependency.

## 2. Schemas and ownership

| Schema | Module | Purpose | Tables (P0 / total) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `platform` | NFR | Outbox, job queue, dedupe, idempotency, runtime config, search index | 8 / 8 |
| `identity` | AUTH | Users, sign-in methods, sessions, roles, profiles, enrolments, consent, reference lists | 18 / 22 |
| `community` | COM | Moderation; later threads, mentors, forums, groups | 4 / 13 |
| `notify` | PLAN | SMS log, inbox, push, preferences; later email, calendar, tracker, eligibility, channels | 6 / 14 |
| `ops` | OPS | Audit log, banners, notices; later flags, campaigns, experiments | 3 / 8 |
| `curriculum` | TAX | Tracks, syllabus versions, taxonomy, exam dates, boards | 6 / 7 |
| `qbank` | QB | Questions, versions, content, rubrics, papers, notes, imports, dedupe; later families, stats, flashcards, current affairs | 17 / 24 |
| `cms` | CMS | Workflow, blind verification, assignments, error reports, corrections, AI review items; later AI drafts, author pay, media library | 7 / 11 |
| `practice` | PRAC | Sessions, attempts, adjustments, notebook, bookmarks, notes; later ability, reviews, daily mix, offline packs, duels | 8 / 17 |
| `exam` | EXAM | Blueprints, exams, papers, registrations, attempts, answer log, results, merit, control room; later integrity, series, certificates, proctoring, predictions | 20 / 33 |
| `analytics` | ANL | Topic progress, daily study, projection versions, rebuild audits; later mastery, trends, percentiles, forecasts, report exports | 4 / 10 |
| `ai` | AI | Threads, messages, citations, usage, canonical cache, sources, chunks, evals, photo solves, feedback, grading; later AI items, summaries, coach, misconceptions, voice | 19 / 27 |
| `rewards` | GAM | Rules, ledger, totals, daily activity, streaks, friends, board flags, snapshots; later badges, leagues, challenges, share cards, store, follows, teams | 10 / 24 |
| `commerce` | PAY | Catalogue, entitlements, quotas, orders, payments, refunds, coupons, referrals, invoices, reconciliation; later subscriptions, family, affiliates, sponsorship | 20 / 31 |
| `institution` | INST | Teacher approval, batches, written tasks, submissions; later workspaces, rosters, seats, homework, OMR, paper generation, marketplace, white-label, attendance | 7 / 23 |
| `parent` | PAR | Guardian links, codes, controls, weekly reports, alerts | 0 / 6 |
| **Total** | | | **157 / 278** |

The first count is tables created in Builds 1–6 (the P0 backend); the second is all nine builds. Counts are computed from §3; [`16_feature_traceability_matrix.md`](16_feature_traceability_matrix.md) lists the exact tables per feature.

## 3. Complete table inventory

Columns: **B** = build that creates the table. **Key columns** lists the identity and the columns other modules or indexes depend on; full column definitions are in §4. "P" marks partitioned tables.

### 3.1 `platform` (NFR)

| Table | B | Features | Purpose | Key columns and constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `outbox` P | 1 | NFR-09 | Events written in the producer's transaction; relayed to S3 log and job queue | PK `(id, recorded_on)`; `event_type`, `schema_version`, `aggregate_type`, `aggregate_id`, `user_id`, `track_enrolment_id`, `tenant_id`, `payload jsonb`, `occurred_at`, `recorded_at`, `relayed_at`; partial ix `(recorded_at) WHERE relayed_at IS NULL` |
| `jobs` | 1 | NFR-09, all workers | Postgres job queue (`FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`) | `id`, `kind`, `queue` (canonical list in `core/jobs/queues.py`), `priority smallint` (−100..100), `status` (queued, running, done, failed), `payload`, `run_at`, `attempts`, `max_attempts DEFAULT 8`, `locked_by`, `locked_until`, `last_error`, `dedupe_key` (uq partial where not null), `dedupe_until`; ix `(queue, priority DESC, run_at) WHERE status = 'queued'` |
| `dead_jobs` | 1 | NFR-08 | Jobs that exhausted retries; alert source | `id`, `original_job_id`, `kind`, `payload`, `attempts`, `last_error`, `failed_at`, `resolved_at`, `resolved_by` |
| `processed_events` P | 1 | NFR-09 | Consumer-side dedupe: one row per (consumer, event) | PK `(consumer, event_id, event_recorded_on)` |
| `idempotency_keys` | 1 | NFR-06, all POSTs | Replay protection for non-answer POSTs; 7-day retention | PK `(user_id, key)`; `request_hash`, `method`, `route`, `status_code`, `response jsonb`, `resource_id`, `created_at`, `expires_at` |
| `runtime_config` | 1 | OPS-07 (P0 part), OFF-01 | Kill switches (tutor, photo solve, registrations, payments), min app version, support links | PK `key`; `value jsonb`, `description`, `updated_at`, `updated_by` |
| `relay_checkpoints` | 1 | NFR-09 | Progress markers for the S3 relay and Parquet flushes | PK `relay_name`; `last_recorded_at`, `last_event_id`, `updated_at` |
| `search_documents` | 2 (vector column in 4) | NFR-04, CMS-05, AI-02, AI-03 | One row per published question version and language; lexical, Banglish, maths and vector fields | `id`, `entity_type`, `entity_id`, `question_id`, `question_version_id`, `lang`, `tenant_id`, `syllabus_version_ids uuid[]`, `node_ids uuid[]`, `status`, `tsv tsvector`, `folded_text`, `banglish_text`, `math_tokens text[]`, `embedding vector(1024)` (B4), `embedding_model`, `metadata jsonb`; GIN on `tsv`, `node_ids`, `math_tokens`; GIN trigram on `folded_text`, `banglish_text`; HNSW on `embedding` (B4); uq `(entity_type, entity_id, lang)` |

|---|---|---|---|---|

| `consent_documents` | 1 | NFR-07 | Versioned consent and privacy text | PK `(version, lang)`; `body_sha256`, `published_at` |

| `data_requests` | 1 (support flow 6, automation 9) | NFR-07, AUTH-10, AUTH-01 (Meta data deletion) | Export and deletion requests, 30-day due date | `id`, `user_id`, `kind` (export, deletion), `status`, `requested_via`, `due_at`, `completed_at`, `handled_by`, `artifact_key`, `module_status jsonb` (per-module completion from events), `confirmation_code` |
| `device_profile_links` | 8 | AUTH-08 | Several accounts on one shared device | PK `(device_id, user_id)`; `added_at`, `removed_at` |

| `email_verifications` | 8 | AUTH-01 | Email verification tokens | `id`, `user_id`, `email_hash`, `token_hash` (uq), `expires_at`, `consumed_at` |
| `apple_notifications` | 8 | AUTH-01 (Apple) | Apple server-to-server events (consent revoked, account deleted) | `id`, `event_id` (uq), `kind`, `provider_subject`, `received_at`, `processed_at` |

### 3.17 Redis keyspace (not a system of record)

Redis is never the system of record. There are **two Redis deployments** (R-09):

- **General Redis** (ElastiCache, `allkeys-lru` permitted for caches): rate limits, quotas, caches, leaderboards and the telemetry stream. Everything here can be rebuilt from Postgres or is safe to lose.
- **Exam Redis**: a separate replication group, Multi-AZ with automatic failover, `maxmemory-policy noeviction`, AOF `appendfsync everysec`. It holds every `exam:*` key and `exam_lock:*`, so telemetry bursts, cache churn or eviction cannot touch the exam path. It holds the **ack ledger** (`exam:acks`, `exam:sent`) as a durable-enough cache. The ledger is backed by the `acked_through` carried in every SQS message, the submit marker and client evidence; if it is lost, ingest accepts a client's claim once (`resync`) and the contiguity check falls back to the other evidence ([`09_build_05.md`](09_build_05.md) §C.1 step 13).

Every key family below has a rebuild or fallback path.

| Key pattern | Owner | B | Type, TTL | Rebuilt from |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `rl:{scope}:{id}:{window}` | NFR | 1 | counter, window TTL | Not rebuilt (safe to lose) |
| `otp:send:{phone_hash}` | AUTH | 1 | counter, 15 min and 24 h | Safe to lose; `otp_challenges` is the fallback count |
| `nonce:social:{nonce}` | AUTH | 1 | string, 5 min | Not rebuilt; sign-in retried |
| `jwks:{provider}` | AUTH | 1 | string, provider cache headers | Refetched |
| `auth:grants:{user_id}` | AUTH | 1 | hash of scoped grants, 60 s | `identity.role_grants` |
| `idem:lock:{user_id}:{key}` | NFR | 1 | string, 30 s | In-flight marker for a request with that idempotency key; safe to lose |
| `revoked_sess:{session_id}` | AUTH | 1 | string, until access token expiry (15 min) | `device_sessions.revoked_at` |
| `cfg:runtime` | NFR | 1 | hash, 30 s | `platform.runtime_config` |
| `pay:access:{user_id}` | PAY | 3 | JSON of active entitlements, 30 s | `commerce.entitlements` |
| `prac:session:{session_id}` | PRAC | 3 | JSON session manifest, until completion + 1 h | `practice_sessions`, `session_items` |
| `qv:{question_version_id}:{lang}` | QB | 2 | JSON payload (immutable), 7 days | `qbank.question_versions`, `qv_content` |
| `tax:tree:{syllabus_version_id}` | TAX | 2 | JSON tree, until a node changes | `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes` |
| `lb:marker:{board}` | GAM | 3 | string (last applied ledger position), none | Rebuilt with the board |
| `quota:{user}:{feature}:{period}` | PAY | 3 | counter, period TTL | `ai_usage` counts and `attempt_answers` counts for the period |
| `lb:{period}:{period_key}:{track}:{audience}:{audience_id}` | GAM | 3 | sorted set; weekly 35 days, monthly 400 days, all-time none | `reward_transactions` by `recorded_at` (rebuild job per board) |
| `gam:usual_hour:{user}` | GAM | 3 | string, 7 days | `attempt_answers` |
| `exam_lock:{user_id}` (exam Redis) | EXAM (written), AI (read) | 4 contract / 5 writer | string, until exam end + max extension + grace | **Cache only.** The authority is `exam.is_user_exam_locked`: an in-process running-exam set refreshed every 15 s plus per-user registration membership cached 15 s. A missing key never unlocks; with Postgres and Redis both down it fails closed for the last-known registry (R-22). |
| `ai:cb:{provider}` | AI | 4 | hash, rolling 30 s | Not rebuilt (breaker restarts closed-but-probing) |
| `ai:budget:{dhaka_date}` | AI | 4 | counter, 48 h | `ai.cost_budgets`, `ai_usage` |
| `ai:inflight:{user}` | AI | 4 | counter, 60 s | Safe to lose (admission control) |
| `exam:acks:{attempt_id}` (exam Redis) | EXAM | 5 | int (highest acknowledged batch `seq`), exam end + 1 day | Ack ledger written by ingest after SQS success; fallback = `acked_through` in messages, submit marker, client report (`resync` once) |
| `exam:sent:{exam_id}` (exam Redis) | EXAM | 5 | counter of new batches sent to SQS, exam end + 7 days | Aggregate contiguity check; if lost, degraded-evidence incident note |
| `exam:resync:{attempt_id}`, `exam:client_ack:{attempt_id}` (exam Redis) | EXAM | 5 | flag / int, exam end + 1 day | Recovery evidence for the contiguity check |
| `exam:result:{attempt_id}` | EXAM | 5 | JSON string, 30 days | `exam_results` + `merit_entries` |
| `exam:status:{exam_id}` | EXAM | 5 | hash (ends_at, extensions, status), exam end + 1 day | `exams`, `time_extensions` |
| `quota_charged:{message_id}` | AI | 4 | string, 48 h | Charge-once marker (§5.2 R17); reconciled nightly against `ai_usage` |
| `ai:canon:{qv_id}:{action}:{option}:{lang}:{prompt_version}` | AI | 4 | string, 7 days | `ai.canonical_answers` |
| `ai:qemb:{sha256}` | AI | 4 | bytes, 1 day | Recomputed embedding |
| `exam:epoch:{attempt_id}` | EXAM | 5 | int, exam end + 1 day | `exam_attempts.epoch` |
| `exam:inflight:{attempt_id}` | EXAM | 5 | string (seq), 30 s | Safe to lose (one batch in flight) |
| `exam:live:{exam_id}` | EXAM | 5 | HyperLogLog of attempts seen, exam end + 1 day | Control-room count; advisory |
| `exam:paper:{exam_id}:{lang}` | EXAM | 5 | hash (bundle URL, sha256), exam end + 1 day | `exam.exam_papers` |
| `ops:banners:{placement}` | OPS | 6 | JSON, 60 s | `ops.banners` |
| `pay:bkash:token` | PAY | 6 | string, provider token TTL − 60 s | Re-fetched from bKash |
| `crawl:{user}:{hour}` | NFR | 6 | HyperLogLog of question ids, 2 h | Safe to lose |
| `telemetry:stream` | NFR | 3 | Redis stream, `MAXLEN ~ 2,000,000` | Client telemetry is best-effort; flushed to S3 Parquet every 60 s |

---

## 4. Per-table specifications

<!-- §4 is assembled from the per-schema specification parts. Each entry follows the template in §4.0. -->

### 4.0 Template

Every table entry contains: **Why** it exists · **Owner** · **Features** · **Relations** (with cardinality) · **Columns** (name, type, null, default, constraint) · **Indexes** (and the query each serves) · **Read/write pattern** (rate at Stage 1 and Stage 4, §5.11) · **Scalability risks** and mitigation · **Build**.

### 4.1 `platform`

Shared notes: `platform` is owned by NFR. The other modules reach it only through `app.modules.nfr` (`publish`, `enqueue`, `idempotent`, `runtime_config`) and never import its models. Stage 1 means about 1k concurrent users and Stage 4 about 100k+ ([`17`](17_scalability_and_performance.md)).

#### `platform.outbox`
- **Why:** Lets an event be written atomically with the state change that caused it, so nothing committed goes unpublished and nothing unpublished is relayed (v2 §3.5).
- **Owner:** NFR.
- **Features:** NFR-09, plus every event-driven feature.
- **Relations:** None by FK. `aggregate_id`, `user_id` and `tenant_id` are soft references.
- **Read/write:** Each producer transaction inserts 1–3 rows. The relay reads unrelayed rows in batches of 1,000 and marks them relayed. Rates come from the single table in [`17`](17_scalability_and_performance.md) §2.2: about 25 rows/s at Stage 1 and about 2,500 rows/s at Stage 4.
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `(id, recorded_on)`.
  - Partial `ix_outbox_unrelayed (recorded_at) WHERE relayed_at IS NULL`: the relay scan.
  - `(aggregate_type, aggregate_id)`: debugging and replay of one aggregate.
- **Risks and mitigations:**
  - Update churn bloats the table. Mitigations: daily partitions, drop after 7 days, fill factor 80.
  - The relay can fall behind at Stage 4. Mitigation: parallel relays, each owning partitions by `hash(id) % N`.
- **Build:** 1.
- **Partitioning:** `PARTITION BY RANGE (recorded_on)`, one partition per day.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK part (event id, UUIDv7) |
| recorded_on | date | no | — | PK part; partition key; Dhaka-independent UTC date of `recorded_at` |
| event_type | text | no | — | `CHECK (event_type ~ '^[a-z_]+\.[a-z_.]+$')` |
| schema_version | smallint | no | 1 | `CHECK (schema_version > 0)` |
| aggregate_type | text | no | — | |
| aggregate_id | uuid | no | — | |
| user_id | uuid | yes | — | soft ref |
| track_enrolment_id | uuid | yes | — | soft ref |
| tenant_id | uuid | yes | — | |
| payload | jsonb | no | — | envelope payload (v2 §3.3) |
| occurred_at | timestamptz | no | — | effective or business time |
| recorded_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| relayed_at | timestamptz | yes | — | set by the relay |
| relay_attempts | smallint | no | 0 | |

#### `platform.jobs`
- **Why:** The Postgres job queue that runs all background work except exam answers (v2 C9).
- **Owner:** NFR.
- **Features:** NFR-09, plus every worker job.
- **Relations:** None.
- **Read/write:**
  - Writes: enqueued by the relay and by services.
  - Claims: `SELECT … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED LIMIT n` ordered by priority and `run_at`.
  - Completed jobs **without** a `dedupe_key` are deleted on completion. Jobs **with** a `dedupe_key` are kept with `status = 'done'` until `dedupe_until`, so the key keeps blocking re-enqueues for its window (for example one reminder per user per day); the retention job deletes them after `dedupe_until`.
  - Rates: see the single derived rate table in [`17`](17_scalability_and_performance.md) §2. Because the relay creates one consumer job per batch of up to 500 events (§5.12), job inserts stay near 15/s at Stage 4.
  - v2 §0.2 sets a review point at 1k jobs/s per queue.
- **Indexes:**
  - `ix_jobs_claim (queue, priority DESC, run_at) WHERE status = 'queued'`: claim.
  - `ix_jobs_expired_lease (locked_until) WHERE locked_until IS NOT NULL`: lease recovery.
  - Unique partial `(dedupe_key) WHERE dedupe_key IS NOT NULL`: one job per key for as long as the row exists. `now()` cannot appear in an index predicate, so the window is enforced by the retention job deleting rows whose `dedupe_until` has passed (and failed rows move to `dead_jobs`).
- **Risks and mitigations:**
  - Hot-table vacuum. Mitigations: per-table autovacuum (`scale_factor 0.01`), fill factor 70, deletion on completion.
  - One queue starving the others. Mitigation: one `queue` per workload class, each with its own worker concurrency.
- **Canonical queues [Decision, R-04]:** defined once in `core/jobs/queues.py` as `QUEUES = ("default", "relay", "notify", "sms", "gam", "rescoring", "imports", "ai", "exam", "campaign", "payments", "maintenance")`. The migration builds the `queue` CHECK from that tuple, and the contract test `tests/contract/test_job_queues.py` asserts that the database CHECK equals `queues.py`. No other queue name may appear in code or documents. OTP send uses queue `sms` at priority 100.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| kind | text | no | — | handler name, e.g. `gam.leaderboard.apply` |
| queue | text | no | 'default' | `CHECK (queue IN ('default','relay','notify','sms','gam','rescoring','imports','ai','exam','campaign','payments','maintenance'))`, generated from `core/jobs/queues.py` |
| priority | smallint | no | 0 | `CHECK (priority BETWEEN -100 AND 100)` |
| status | text | no | 'queued' | `CHECK (status IN ('queued','running','done','failed'))` |
| dedupe_until | timestamptz | yes | — | End of the dedupe window for a keyed job; the row is kept as `done` until then |
| payload | jsonb | no | '{}' | |
| run_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| attempts | smallint | no | 0 | |
| max_attempts | smallint | no | 8 | `CHECK (max_attempts BETWEEN 1 AND 20)` |
| locked_by | text | yes | — | worker id |
| locked_until | timestamptz | yes | — | lease |
| last_error | text | yes | — | truncated to 4 KB |
| dedupe_key | text | yes | — | unique partial |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `platform.dead_jobs`
- **Why:** Keeps jobs that exhausted their retries for inspection, replay and alerting.
- **Owner:** NFR.
- **Features:** NFR-08, NFR-09.
- **Relations:** `original_job_id` is a soft reference to a deleted `jobs` row.
- **Read/write:** Rare inserts. Admin reads.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(kind, failed_at DESC)`: dashboard.
  - Partial `(failed_at) WHERE resolved_at IS NULL`: alert on any unresolved row.
- **Risks:** None material.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| original_job_id | uuid | no | — | |
| kind | text | no | — | |
| queue | text | no | — | |
| payload | jsonb | no | — | |
| attempts | smallint | no | — | |
| last_error | text | no | — | |
| failed_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| resolved_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| resolved_by | uuid | yes | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| resolution | text | yes | — | `CHECK (resolution IN ('replayed','discarded','fixed_manually'))` |

#### `platform.processed_events`
- **Why:** Consumer-side dedupe. A consumer's write and this row commit together, so a redelivered job becomes a no-op.
- **Owner:** NFR.
- **Features:** NFR-09.
- **Relations:** `event_id` is a soft reference to `outbox.id` for single-event consumers. For batched consumer jobs (the default, §5.12) the dedupe unit is the relay batch: `event_id` holds the batch's first event id, and `relay_batch_id` holds the deterministic `first_event_id:last_event_id` for that consumer. Per-event effects inside a batch stay idempotent through ledger keys and the leaderboard markers (§5.2 R20).
- **Read/write:** One insert per consumer job, about 15 rows/s at Stage 4 ([`17`](17_scalability_and_performance.md) §2.2), instead of one per event.
- **Indexes:** PK only (`INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`).
- **Risks and mitigations:** Volume. Mitigation: daily partitions, dropped after 14 days.
- **Build:** 1.
- **Partitioning:** `PARTITION BY RANGE (event_recorded_on)`, daily.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| consumer | text | no | — | PK part |
| event_id | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| event_recorded_on | date | no | — | PK part; partition key; copied from the envelope (first event of the batch) |
| relay_batch_id | text | yes | — | `first_event_id:last_event_id` for batched jobs; NULL for single-event jobs |
| processed_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `platform.idempotency_keys`
- **Why:** Replay protection for POSTs other than answers and exam batches (v2 §3.5).
- **Owner:** NFR.
- **Features:** NFR-06, plus all R-marked endpoints in [`14`](14_api_contracts.md).
- **Relations:** `user_id` → `identity.users`, many-to-one. It is a soft reference for anonymous callers, whose `user_id` is the device-hash UUID.
- **Read/write:** One read and one write per idempotent POST. Stage 4: about 500/s.
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `(user_id, key)`.
  - `(expires_at)`: purge.
- **Risks:** Large JSON responses. Mitigation: responses over 64 KB store only `resource_id` and are re-read on replay.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK part (no FK: anonymous device scope allowed) |
| key | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| request_hash | bytea | no | — | SHA-256 of method + route + canonical body |
| method | text | no | — | `CHECK (method IN ('POST','PUT','PATCH','DELETE'))` |
| route | text | no | — | route template |
| status_code | smallint | yes | — | null while in flight |
| response | jsonb | yes | — | |
| resource_id | uuid | yes | — | |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| expires_at | timestamptz | no | now() + interval '7 days' | |

#### `platform.runtime_config`
- **Why:** Holds kill switches (tutor, photo solve, registrations, payments, social providers), the minimum app version and the support links. All can change without a deploy (v2 §7.5, §8.3).
- **Owner:** NFR. Written only through OPS `audited`.
- **Features:** OPS-07 (P0 part), OFF-01, AI-05.
- **Relations:** `updated_by` → `identity.users`.
- **Read/write:** Read through a 30 s Redis and process cache. Writes are rare.
- **Indexes:** PK.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| key | text | no | — | PK; `CHECK (key ~ '^[a-z0-9_.]+$')` |
| value | jsonb | no | — | validated against a per-key schema in code |
| description | text | no | — | |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| updated_by | uuid | yes | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |

#### `platform.relay_checkpoints`
- **Why:** Durable progress markers for the outbox relays and for the Parquet flushers of the S3 event log.
- **Owner:** NFR.
- **Features:** NFR-09.
- **Relations:** None.
- **Read/write:** Updated once per relay batch.
- **Indexes:** PK.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| relay_name | text | no | — | PK |
| last_recorded_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| last_event_id | uuid | yes | — | |
| s3_object_key | text | yes | — | last Parquet object written |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `platform.search_documents`
- **Why:** One search index row per published question version and language. It serves Bangla full text, phonetic folding, Banglish, maths tokens and vector search on Postgres (v2 §7.1).
- **Owner:** NFR.
- **Features:** NFR-04, CMS-05, AI-02, AI-03.
- **Relations:**
  - `question_version_id` → `qbank.question_versions`, many-to-one (one row per language).
  - `question_id` → `qbank.questions`.
- **Read/write:**
  - Writes: rebuilt by the index job on `question.published` and `question.corrected`. A 150k-question bank in 2 languages is about 300k rows.
  - Reads: search queries go to the read replica. Stage 1: about 20 queries/s. Stage 4: about 400 queries/s.
- **Indexes:**
  - GIN `tsv`: lexical search.
  - GIN trigram on `folded_text` and on `banglish_text`: spelling variants and Latin-script queries.
  - GIN on `math_tokens`: maths search.
  - GIN on `node_ids` and on `syllabus_version_ids`: taxonomy filters.
  - HNSW `embedding vector_cosine_ops` (`m=16, ef_construction=64`), from B4: semantic search.
  - B-tree `(tenant_id, status)`: permission filter.
  - Unique `(entity_type, entity_id, lang)`.
- **Risks and mitigations:**
  - HNSW build memory and filtered-ANN recall: filters on HNSW run after the index scan, so a selective filter loses recall. **Mitigations (R-23):**
    - Require pgvector ≥ 0.8 with `SET LOCAL hnsw.iterative_scan = relaxed_order` and a tuned `hnsw.ef_search` (100 by default, more for filtered queries) plus `hnsw.max_scan_tuples`.
    - Tenant-private rows use a partial HNSW index per large tenant, or exact search for small tenants (B8).
    - The 500-query gate includes filtered queries for one chapter and one tenant. OpenSearch is the move if search p95 exceeds 300 ms or the index passes 5M documents (v2 §0.2).
- **Build:** 2. The `embedding` column and the HNSW index come in 4.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| entity_type | text | no | 'question_version' | `CHECK (entity_type IN ('question_version','concept_note','paper'))` |
| entity_id | uuid | no | — | |
| question_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `qbank.questions(id)` |
| question_version_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `qbank.question_versions(id)` |
| lang | text | no | — | `CHECK (lang IN ('bn','en'))` |
| tenant_id | uuid | yes | — | |
| syllabus_version_ids | uuid[] | no | '{}' | |
| node_ids | uuid[] | no | '{}' | |
| question_type | text | yes | — | |
| difficulty | smallint | yes | — | |
| source_kind | text | yes | — | board, admission, job, school |
| source_year | smallint | yes | — | |
| status | text | no | 'active' | `CHECK (status IN ('active','retired'))` |
| tsv | tsvector | no | — | `simple` configuration over normalised text |
| folded_text | text | no | — | phonetic skeleton |
| banglish_text | text | no | — | romanised and folded |
| math_tokens | text[] | no | '{}' | canonical LaTeX sub-expressions |
| embedding | vector(1024) | yes | — | bge-m3 (B4) |
| metadata | jsonb | no | '{}' | display snippet |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

### 4.2 `identity`

Shared notes: AUTH owns this schema. PII columns follow §1: `*_enc` is AES-256-GCM ciphertext and `*_hash` is HMAC-SHA256 with a separate key. The application role cannot read the keys; they are injected from the secret store.

#### `identity.users`
- **Why:** The root of every account. A single user can sign in by phone, Google, Facebook, Apple or password.
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-01, AUTH-04, NFR-06, NFR-07, AUTH-10.
- **Relations:**
  - 1–N to `device_sessions`, `external_identities`, `role_grants` and `track_enrolments`.
  - 1–1 to `profiles` and `privacy_settings`.
  - Referenced by every schema (§1.1).
- **Read/write:**
  - Reads: on token refresh, `/v1/me` and admin lookups. The access JWT avoids a read per request.
  - Writes: on sign-up and profile edits. Stage 4: about 50 writes/s.
- **Indexes:**
  - Unique partial `phone_hash WHERE phone_hash IS NOT NULL`: OTP sign-in lookup.
  - Unique partial `email_hash WHERE email_hash IS NOT NULL`: Google auto-link and password sign-in.
  - Partial `(status) WHERE status <> 'active'`: admin queues.
- **Risks:** `last_seen_at` write churn. Mitigation: update at most once per hour per user, through a Redis gate.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| status | text | no | 'active' | `CHECK (status IN ('active','suspended','pending_deletion','deleted'))` |
| phone_enc | bytea | yes | — | |
| phone_hash | bytea | yes | — | unique partial |
| email_enc | bytea | yes | — | |
| email_hash | bytea | yes | — | unique partial |
| email_verified_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| display_name | text | yes | — | `CHECK (char_length(display_name) <= 40)`; moderated |
| first_name | text | yes | — | `CHECK (char_length(first_name) <= 30)`; shown on boards (NFR-07) |
| date_of_birth | date | yes | — | required before consent-gated features |
| locale | text | no | 'bn' | `CHECK (locale IN ('bn','en'))` |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| last_seen_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| deleted_at | timestamptz | yes | — | `CHECK ((status = 'deleted') = (deleted_at IS NOT NULL))` |

#### `identity.external_identities`
- **Why:** Social sign-in identities (Google and Facebook from B1, Apple from B8). Linking follows the safe rules in the plan: no automatic linking on an unverified email.
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-01 (Google, Facebook and Apple sub-rows).
- **Relations:** N–1 to `users`. At most one active identity per provider per user.
- **Read/write:** Read on every social sign-in. Stage 4: about 30/s at the morning peak.
- **Indexes:**
  - Unique `(provider, provider_subject)`: sign-in lookup and the concurrent-first-sign-in race (R16).
  - Unique partial `(user_id, provider) WHERE unlinked_at IS NULL`.
  - `(email_hash) WHERE email_verified`: Google auto-link check.
- **Risks:** None material.
- **Build:** 1 (Apple 8).

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `users(id)` |
| provider | text | no | — | `CHECK (provider IN ('google','facebook','apple'))` |
| provider_subject | text | no | — | Google `sub`, Facebook app-scoped user id, Apple `sub` |
| email_enc | bytea | yes | — | |
| email_hash | bytea | yes | — | |
| email_verified | boolean | no | false | Facebook is always stored false |
| display_name | text | yes | — | |
| linked_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| last_login_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| unlinked_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| unlink_reason | text | yes | — | `CHECK (unlink_reason IN ('user','deauthorized','data_deletion','admin','apple_revoked'))` |

#### `identity.otp_challenges`
- **Why:** Hashed, single-use OTPs with a 5-minute expiry and a 5-try limit, for sign-in, guardian consent and phone change.
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-01, NFR-06, NFR-07.
- **Relations:** Referenced by `guardian_consents.otp_challenge_id`.
- **Read/write:** One insert per send and one update per verify. Stage 4 morning peak: about 100/s.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(phone_hash, created_at DESC)`: latest challenge and the per-phone counts.
  - `(created_at)`: purge after 30 days.
- **Risks:** SMS pumping fraud. Mitigation: T-otp limits, device and IP limits, an alert on cost spikes by number prefix.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| phone_hash | bytea | no | — | |
| purpose | text | no | — | `CHECK (purpose IN ('sign_in','guardian_consent','phone_change'))` |
| code_hash | bytea | no | — | HMAC of the 6-digit code with a per-challenge salt |
| attempts | smallint | no | 0 | `CHECK (attempts BETWEEN 0 AND 5)` |
| expires_at | timestamptz | no | now() + interval '5 min' | |
| consumed_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| ip_hash | bytea | yes | — | |
| device_id | text | yes | — | |
| sms_message_id | uuid | yes | — | soft ref `notify.sms_messages` |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `identity.device_sessions`
- **Why:** Revocable sessions, one per device (AUTH-02). They also carry the epoch for taking over a live exam on another device (v2 §3.4).
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-02, EXAM-04a, EXAM-05.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `users`; 1–N to `refresh_tokens`. Soft-referenced by `exam.exam_attempts.device_session_id`.
- **Read/write:**
  - Reads: on refresh and listing. A revoked session is checked against the Redis `revoked_sess` key, not this table.
  - Writes: on sign-in, refresh (`last_seen_at`) and revoke.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(user_id) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL`: session list.
  - `(device_id)`: shared-device lookups (B8).
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `users(id)` |
| device_id | text | no | — | client-generated install id |
| platform | text | no | — | `CHECK (platform IN ('android','web','ios'))` |
| app_version | text | yes | — | |
| user_agent | text | yes | — | truncated to 256 characters |
| ip_hash | bytea | yes | — | |
| sign_in_method | text | no | — | `CHECK (sign_in_method IN ('otp','google','facebook','apple','password'))` |
| exam_epoch | integer | no | 0 | |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| last_seen_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| revoked_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| revoke_reason | text | yes | — | `CHECK (revoke_reason IN ('user','logout','token_reuse','admin','exam_takeover','account_deleted'))` |

#### `identity.refresh_tokens`
- **Why:** Rotating refresh tokens. Reusing a rotated token revokes the whole token family (R15).
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-02.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `device_sessions`. `parent_id` points to the previous token in the chain (self).
- **Read/write:** Every 15 minutes per active device: about 110 refreshes/s at Stage 4 with 100k concurrent users.
- **Indexes:**
  - Unique `token_hash`: refresh lookup.
  - `(family_id)`: family revoke.
  - `(expires_at)`: purge.
- **Risks:** Volume. Mitigation: rows older than 30 days after expiry are deleted daily.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| session_id | uuid | no | — | FK `device_sessions(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE |
| family_id | uuid | no | — | |
| token_hash | bytea | no | — | unique; SHA-256 of 32 random bytes |
| parent_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `refresh_tokens(id)` |
| issued_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| expires_at | timestamptz | no | — | user tokens 30 days; staff tokens 12 h |
| rotated_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| revoked_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

#### `identity.roles`
- **Why:** The eight roles of AUTH-03 (seeded): student, parent, teacher, institution_admin, content_author, reviewer, ops_admin, super_admin. Two more are added later: mentor (B7) and affiliate (B8).
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-03.
- **Relations:** 1–N to `role_permissions` and `role_grants`.
- **Read/write:** Read-mostly; cached in process.
- **Indexes:** PK.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| code | text | no | — | PK; `CHECK (code ~ '^[a-z_]+$')` |
| name_bn | text | no | — | |
| name_en | text | no | — | |
| is_staff | boolean | no | false | staff roles need an `aud=admin` session |

#### `identity.permissions`
- **Why:** Permission codes checked by `can()` (e.g. `content.publish`, `pay.refund`).
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-03.
- **Relations:** 1–N to `role_permissions`.
- **Read/write:** Seeded by migrations; cached.
- **Indexes:** PK.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| code | text | no | — | PK; `CHECK (code ~ '^[a-z_]+\.[a-z_.]+$')` |
| description | text | no | — | |
| module | text | no | — | owning module code |

#### `identity.role_permissions`
- **Why:** Maps roles to permissions.
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-03.
- **Relations:** N–N between `roles` and `permissions`.
- **Read/write:** Seeded; cached.
- **Indexes:** PK.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| role_code | text | no | — | PK part; FK `roles(code)` |
| permission_code | text | no | — | PK part; FK `permissions(code)` |

#### `identity.role_grants`
- **Why:** Scoped role assignment, e.g. a reviewer for SSC Physics (CMS-03), or a teacher for one batch.
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-03, CMS-03, INST-02a, INST-01.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `users` and to `roles`. `scope_id` is a soft reference whose meaning depends on `scope_type`.
- **Read/write:** Loaded once per sign-in into JWT roles. Scopes are cached per user for 60 s and invalidated by the `role.changed` event.
- **Indexes:**
  - Unique partial `(user_id, role_code, scope_type, scope_id) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL`.
  - `(scope_type, scope_id) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL`: "who can review this subject".
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `users(id)` |
| role_code | text | no | — | FK `roles(code)` |
| scope_type | text | no | 'global' | `CHECK (scope_type IN ('global','track','subject','tenant','batch'))` |
| scope_id | uuid | yes | — | `CHECK ((scope_type = 'global') = (scope_id IS NULL))` |
| granted_by | uuid | yes | — | FK `users(id)` |
| granted_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| revoked_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

#### `identity.profiles`
- **Why:** Student profile fields from AUTH-04. They drive tutor context, leaderboard audiences (district, institution) and eligibility (B8).
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-04, GAM-03, EXAM-06, AI-01, PLAN-05.
- **Relations:**
  - 1–1 to `users`.
  - N–1 to `institutions`, `districts`, `upazilas` and `curriculum.boards`.
- **Read/write:** Read on session start and on tutor requests, cached per user for 5 minutes. Rare writes.
- **Indexes:**
  - PK.
  - `(institution_id)`: institution board rebuild.
  - `(district_id)`: district board rebuild.
- **Risks:** Self-reported institution. Mitigation: the "self-reported" label (C15).
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK; FK `users(id)` |
| class_level | smallint | yes | — | `CHECK (class_level BETWEEN 6 AND 12)` |
| group_code | text | yes | — | `CHECK (group_code IN ('science','business','humanities','general'))` |
| version_lang | text | yes | — | `CHECK (version_lang IN ('bn','en'))` |
| curriculum | text | yes | — | `CHECK (curriculum IN ('general','madrasah','technical'))` |
| institution_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `institutions(id)` |
| district_id | smallint | yes | — | FK `districts(id)` |
| upazila_id | integer | yes | — | FK `upazilas(id)` |
| board_code | text | yes | — | FK `curriculum.boards(code)` (added in B2) |
| exam_year | smallint | yes | — | `CHECK (exam_year BETWEEN 2025 AND 2040)` |
| photo_key | text | yes | — | private storage key |
| photo_status | text | no | 'none' | `CHECK (photo_status IN ('none','pending','approved','rejected'))` |
| reply_lang_pref | text | no | 'auto' | `CHECK (reply_lang_pref IN ('auto','bn','en','banglish'))` |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

- **Why:** Reference list of the 64 districts, used for profiles, district boards and district merit.

#### `identity.institutions`
- **Why:** Reference list of schools, colleges and madrasahs. It allows institution boards and merit at P0, before INST workspaces exist (v2 §7.5).
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-04, GAM-03, EXAM-06.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `districts` and `upazilas`. 1–1 from `institution.workspaces.institution_ref_id` (B8).
- **Read/write:** Search-as-you-type during onboarding. Seeded with about 40k rows **[Assumption]**.
- **Indexes:**
  - Unique partial `eiin WHERE eiin IS NOT NULL`.
  - GIN trigram on `name_bn` and on `name_en`: search.
  - `(district_id)`.
- **Risks:** Duplicate names. Mitigation: `eiin` (the official institution id) where it is known.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |

| name_en | text | no | — | |

| district_id | smallint | no | — | FK `districts(id)` |
| upazila_id | integer | yes | — | FK `upazilas(id)` |
| is_active | boolean | no | true | |

#### `identity.track_enrolments`
- **Why:** Lets one account hold several track enrolments (AUTH-05). Every attempt, reward and board entry carries `track_enrolment_id`. Promotion closes one enrolment and opens the next (AUTH-06).
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** AUTH-05, AUTH-06, PLAN-01, GAM-03.
- **Relations:**
  - N–1 to `users`, `curriculum.tracks` and `curriculum.syllabus_versions`.
  - Self-reference through `promoted_from_id`.
- **Read/write:** Read on every session start, cached for 5 minutes. Rare writes.
- **Indexes:**
  - Unique partial `(user_id, track_id, exam_year) WHERE status = 'active'`.
  - `(user_id, status)`.
  - `(track_id, exam_year) WHERE status = 'active'`: board rebuild and cohorts.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `users(id)` |
| track_id | uuid | no | — | FK `curriculum.tracks(id)` |
| syllabus_version_id | uuid | no | — | FK `curriculum.syllabus_versions(id)` |
| exam_year | smallint | no | — | |
| group_code | text | yes | — | same CHECK list as `profiles.group_code` |
| version_lang | text | no | 'bn' | `CHECK (version_lang IN ('bn','en'))` |
| status | text | no | 'active' | `CHECK (status IN ('active','completed','closed'))` |
| promoted_from_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `track_enrolments(id)` |
| enrolled_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| closed_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

#### `identity.guardian_consents`
- **Why:** The P0 minimum for NFR-07: a guardian's phone is confirmed by OTP and stored as a versioned consent record (v2 §7.5, PDPA s.9).
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** NFR-07, AUTH-10, INST-02a. Consent is required before joining a batch.
- **Relations:**
  - N–1 to `users`.
  - N–1 to `otp_challenges`.
  - Referenced by `parent.guardian_links.consent_id` (B8).
- **Read/write:** One write per minor. Read on consent-gated actions, cached.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(user_id) WHERE withdrawn_at IS NULL`.
  - `(guardian_phone_hash)`: parent link suggestions (B8).
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `users(id)` |
| guardian_phone_enc | bytea | no | — | |
| guardian_phone_hash | bytea | no | — | |
| consent_version | text | no | — | |
| scope | jsonb | no | '{}' | e.g. `{"handwriting_collection": false}` |
| method | text | no | 'otp' | `CHECK (method IN ('otp','in_person','other'))` |
| otp_challenge_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `otp_challenges(id)` |
| granted_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| withdrawn_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

#### `identity.privacy_settings`
- **Why:** Leaderboard opt-out and private-by-default settings (NFR-07, C13).
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** NFR-07, GAM-03, EXAM-06.
- **Relations:** 1–1 with `users`.
- **Read/write:** Read by board rebuilds and the merit render, through the AUTH interface with a cache.
- **Indexes:** PK; partial `(user_id) WHERE leaderboard_visible = false`, used by the filter set.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK; FK `users(id)` |
| leaderboard_visible | boolean | no | true | C13 default |
| opt_out_by | text | yes | — | `CHECK (opt_out_by IN ('self','guardian'))` |
| profile_private | boolean | no | true | |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `identity.data_requests`
- **Why:** Tracks export and deletion requests, with the 30-day due date (v2 §7.5). Meta data-deletion callbacks create rows from B1. Support approves at P0 (B6), and requests are automated in B9 (AUTH-10). Execution is event-driven: AUTH emits `user.deletion_requested`/`user.export_requested`, and each module reports back with `user.deletion_completed`/`user.export_part_ready` into `module_status`. AUTH imports no other module (R-03).
- **Owner:** AUTH.
- **Features:** NFR-07, AUTH-10, AUTH-01 (Meta data deletion).
- **Relations:** N–1 to `users`.
- **Read/write:** Rare.
- **Indexes:** `(status, due_at)`: SLA queue.
- **Risks:** A module that never reports back. Mitigation: an alert at 25 days naming the pending modules.
- **Build:** 1 (table); 6 (flows); 9 (automation).

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `users(id)` |
| kind | text | no | — | `CHECK (kind IN ('export','deletion'))` |
| status | text | no | 'open' | `CHECK (status IN ('open','in_progress','completed','rejected'))` |
| requested_via | text | no | — | `CHECK (requested_via IN ('app','support','guardian','facebook_callback','apple_notification'))` |
| due_at | timestamptz | no | now() + interval '30 days' | |
| completed_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| handled_by | uuid | yes | — | FK `users(id)` |
| artifact_key | text | yes | — | export bundle (signed URL source) |
| module_status | jsonb | no | '{}' | `{module_code: "pending" \| "done" \| "failed"}`, written from completion events |
| confirmation_code | text | yes | — | Meta deletion status code (uq partial where not null) |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

### 4.3 `community`

COM owns this schema. `moderate(text, context)` (B1) is the only synchronous interface the rest of P0 uses.

#### `community.moderation_terms`
- **Why:** The term list behind the Bangla, English and Banglish profanity filter. Terms are stored normalised and folded, so spelling variants match.
- **Owner:** COM.
- **Features:** COM-05.
- **Relations:** None.
- **Read/write:** Loaded into an in-process automaton and refreshed every 5 minutes. Rare admin writes.
- **Indexes:** Unique `(lang, term_folded)`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| lang | text | no | — | `CHECK (lang IN ('bn','en','banglish'))` |
| term_folded | text | no | — | |
| severity | smallint | no | 2 | `CHECK (severity BETWEEN 1 AND 3)` (1 flag, 2 block, 3 block and alert) |
| category | text | no | — | `CHECK (category IN ('profanity','sexual','hate','self_harm','contact_info','spam'))` |
| is_active | boolean | no | true | |
| created_by | uuid | yes | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `community.moderation_decisions`
- **Why:** Audit trail of user text that was blocked or flagged. Used for tuning the filter and for appeals.
- **Owner:** COM.
- **Features:** COM-05, AI-05.
- **Relations:** `target_ref` is a soft reference that depends on `context`.
- **Read/write:** Written only when the verdict is not `allow`. About 1% of moderated texts **[Assumption]**.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(context, created_at DESC)`.
  - `(user_id, created_at DESC)`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| user_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| context | text | no | — | `CHECK (context IN ('display_name','profile_photo','error_report','ticket','tutor_input','tutor_output','post','group_message'))` |
| target_ref | text | yes | — | |
| verdict | text | no | — | `CHECK (verdict IN ('flag','block'))` |
| terms_hit | text[] | no | '{}' | |
| classifier_score | numeric(4,3) | yes | — | |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `community.content_reports`
- **Why:** User reports on content or on users. The P0 report action of COM-05.
- **Owner:** COM.
- **Features:** COM-05, COM-01 (B7), COM-04 (B9).
- **Relations:** N–1 to `identity.users` (the reporter). The target is polymorphic and soft-referenced.
- **Read/write:** Low volume. Read from the admin queue.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(status, created_at)`: queue.
  - `(target_type, target_id)`: merge duplicate reports.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| reporter_user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| target_type | text | no | — | `CHECK (target_type IN ('user','display_name','post','thread','group_message','tutor_message'))` |
| target_id | text | no | — | |
| reason | text | no | — | `CHECK (reason IN ('abuse','spam','sexual','self_harm','privacy','cheating','other'))` |
| text | text | yes | — | `CHECK (char_length(text) <= 1000)`; moderated |
| status | text | no | 'open' | `CHECK (status IN ('open','actioned','dismissed'))` |
| resolved_by | uuid | yes | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| resolved_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `community.user_restrictions`
- **Why:** Mute and ban (COM-05). Checked on write paths that accept user-generated text.
- **Owner:** COM.
- **Features:** COM-05.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `identity.users`.
- **Read/write:** Read through `com.is_restricted(user, scope)`, cached for 60 s.
- **Indexes:** `(user_id) WHERE lifted_at IS NULL`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 1.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |

| id | uuid | no | — | PK |

#### `community.threads`
- **Why:** Discussion threads attached to a question (COM-01), and forum topics (COM-03, B9).
- **Owner:** COM.
- **Features:** COM-01, COM-03.
- **Relations:**
  - At most one question thread per `qbank.questions` row, enforced by a unique partial index.
  - 1–N to `posts`.
- **Read/write:** Read-heavy on question screens. Cached for 30 s.
- **Indexes:**
  - Unique partial `(question_id) WHERE scope = 'question'`.
  - `(node_id, scope, created_at DESC)`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 7.

#### `community.posts`
- **Why:** Replies in a thread. Answers from verified teachers are pinned.
- **Owner:** COM.
- **Features:** COM-01, COM-03.

- **Read/write:** Moderated on write. Read paginated.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(thread_id, status, created_at)`.
  - `(author_id, created_at DESC)`.
- **Risks:** Hot `upvotes` counter. Mitigation: votes are aggregated asynchronously every 30 s.

### 4.4 `notify`

PLAN owns this schema. Other modules call `plan.notify(user_id, category, template_code, data, dedupe_event_id)` or emit events that PLAN consumes. The inbox is the record; push is best effort (v2 §7.4).

#### `notify.sms_messages`
- **Why:** SMS transport log for OTPs, receipts and (from B8) alerts. Also the cost record, because Bangla SMS costs 2 segments.
- **Owner:** PLAN.
- **Features:** AUTH-01, PAY-04, PLAN-06, PAR-02.
- **Relations:** Soft reference from `identity.otp_challenges.sms_message_id`.
- **Read/write:** Stage 1: about 1/s. Exam lobby: avoided by the push sent at T−24 h.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(to_phone_hash, created_at DESC)`.
  - `(status, created_at) WHERE status IN ('queued','sent')`: delivery-receipt reconciliation.
- **Risks:** Cost spikes. Mitigation: an alert on spend per hour by prefix.
- **Build:** 1.
- **Partitioning:** `PARTITION BY RANGE (created_on)`, monthly; 24-month retention.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| created_on | date | no | — | PK part; partition key |
| to_phone_hash | bytea | no | — | |
| to_phone_enc | bytea | no | — | |
| template_code | text | no | — | |
| lang | text | no | 'bn' | `CHECK (lang IN ('bn','en'))` |
| segments | smallint | no | — | `CHECK (segments BETWEEN 1 AND 10)` |
| provider | text | no | — | |
| provider_msg_id | text | yes | — | |
| status | text | no | 'queued' | `CHECK (status IN ('queued','sent','delivered','failed'))` |
| cost_poisha | bigint | yes | — | `CHECK (cost_poisha >= 0)` |
| purpose | text | no | — | `CHECK (purpose IN ('otp','guardian_otp','receipt','alert','report','campaign'))` |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| sent_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| delivered_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| error | text | yes | — | |

#### `notify.email_messages`

- **Why:** Email transport log for password reset, email verification (AUTH-01 email/password) and email campaigns (OPS-05). It mirrors `notify.sms_messages`.
- **Owner:** PLAN.
- **Features:** AUTH-01 (email/password), OPS-05.
- **Relations:** soft reference to the user through `to_email_hash`; optional link to a campaign send.
- **Read/write pattern:** Stage 1 below 1 write/s; campaign bursts up to about 200/s, throttled by the provider rate.
- **Indexes:** PK `(id, created_on)`; `ix_email_status (status, created_at)` serves the retry job; `ix_email_to (to_email_hash, created_at DESC)` serves support lookup.
- **Scalability risks:** Campaign volume. It uses the `campaign` queue so transactional mail is never delayed (v2 §2.2). Monthly partitions, kept 24 months.
- **Build:** 8.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| created_on | date | no | — | PK part, partition key (monthly) |
| to_email_hash | bytea | no | — | HMAC lookup hash |
| to_email_enc | bytea | no | — | Envelope-encrypted address |
| template_code | text | no | — | — |
| lang | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('bn','en') |
| provider | text | no | — | — |
| provider_msg_id | text | yes | — | — |
| status | text | no | 'queued' | CHECK IN ('queued','sent','delivered','bounced','failed') |
| purpose | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('verification','password_reset','receipt','campaign','notice') |
| sent_at | timestamptz | yes | — | — |
| error | text | yes | — | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

| code | text | no | — | PK part |

#### `notify.notifications`
- **Why:** The in-app notification centre (PLAN-02). It is the system of record for every notice: streak, exam reminders, results, corrections (CMS-04a), payments and new content.
- **Owner:** PLAN.
- **Features:** PLAN-02, CMS-04a, EXAM-04, PAY-04.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `identity.users`; 1–N to `deliveries`.
- **Read/write:**
  - Writes: Stage 1 about 3/s. Bulk at result publication: 200k rows in about 5 minutes for a national exam, about 700/s.
  - Reads: the inbox list.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(user_id, created_at DESC)`: inbox, on each partition.
  - `(user_id) WHERE read_at IS NULL`: unread count.
- **Risks:** Bulk-insert spikes. Mitigation: `COPY`-style batched inserts of 5,000 per transaction in the cohort send job.
- **Build:** 3.
- **Partitioning:** `PARTITION BY RANGE (created_on)`, monthly; 6-month retention.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| created_on | date | no | — | PK part; partition key |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| category | text | no | — | `CHECK (category IN ('streak','exam','result','correction','payment','content','system','social','guardian','teacher'))` |
| title_bn | text | no | — | |
| title_en | text | no | — | |
| body_bn | text | no | — | |
| body_en | text | no | — | |
| deep_link | text | yes | — | `CHECK (deep_link ~ '^app://')` |
| data | jsonb | no | '{}' | |
| source_event_id | uuid | yes | — | dedupe goes through `processed_events` |
| is_critical | boolean | no | false | critical notices bypass quiet hours |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| read_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| expires_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

| channel | text | no | — | PK part; `CHECK (channel IN ('whatsapp','telegram'))` |

| verified_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| revoked_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

### 4.5 `ops`

OPS owns this schema. `ops.audited(...)` (B1) is the only way any module performs an admin write (v2 §7.4).

#### `ops.audit_log`
- **Why:** Append-only audit of every admin action and every security event (OPS-04). Records actor, action, target, before and after, reason and IP.
- **Owner:** OPS.
- **Features:** OPS-04, plus every admin write; NFR-06 security events.
- **Relations:** Soft references only. The target is polymorphic.
- **Read/write:**
  - Writes: Stage 1 about 2/s, including security events. Exam control-room bursts are small.
  - Reads: admin search.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(target_type, target_id, occurred_at DESC)`: history of one entity.
  - `(actor_user_id, occurred_at DESC)`: "what did this admin do".
  - `(action, occurred_at DESC)`.
- **Risks:** Tampering. Mitigations: `UPDATE` and `DELETE` are revoked from the application role, and a monthly digest hash goes to S3 Object Lock.
- **Build:** 1.
- **Partitioning:** `PARTITION BY RANGE (occurred_on)`, monthly; 3-year retention.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| occurred_on | date | no | — | PK part; partition key |
| occurred_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| actor_user_id | uuid | yes | — | null for system actors |
| actor_kind | text | no | 'staff' | `CHECK (actor_kind IN ('staff','user','system','provider'))` |
| actor_roles | text[] | no | '{}' | |
| action | text | no | — | `CHECK (action ~ '^[a-z_]+\.[a-z_.]+$')` e.g. `pay.refund.create` |
| module | text | no | — | |
| target_type | text | no | — | |
| target_id | text | no | — | |
| before | jsonb | yes | — | changed columns only; PII as hashes |
| after | jsonb | yes | — | |
| reason | text | yes | — | required for refunds, grants, extensions and invalidations (checked in `audited`) |
| ip_hash | bytea | yes | — | |
| request_id | text | yes | — | |
| tenant_id | uuid | yes | — | |

#### `ops.banners`

### 4.6 `curriculum`

TAX owns this schema. Every tree is data, not code (TAX-01). Reads are served through `tax.get_tree()` and `tax.get_node_path()`, with an immutable cache keyed by `syllabus_version_id` and a version stamp. Public endpoints are edge-cached.

#### `curriculum.tracks`
- **Why:** Academic, admission, job, madrasah and technical tracks, each stored as a row (TAX-02, 02a, 02b, 05).
- **Owner:** TAX.
- **Features:** TAX-01, TAX-02, TAX-02a, TAX-02b, TAX-05.
- **Relations:** 1–N to `syllabus_versions`, `track_levels` and `track_exam_dates`. Referenced by enrolments, products and boards.
- **Read/write:** Read-only in practice.
- **Indexes:** Unique `code`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| code | text | no | — | unique, e.g. `ssc`, `hsc`, `du-ka`, `bcs-prelim` |
| kind | text | no | — | `CHECK (kind IN ('academic','admission','job','madrasah','technical'))` |
| name_bn | text | no | — | |
| name_en | text | no | — | |
| sort | integer | no | 0 | |
| icon | text | yes | — | |
| is_active | boolean | no | true | |

#### `curriculum.track_levels`
- **Why:** The configurable hierarchy levels per track (TAX-01). For example: track, exam or class, group or unit, subject, paper, chapter, topic, sub-topic.
- **Owner:** TAX.
- **Features:** TAX-01.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `tracks`.
- **Read/write:** Static.
- **Indexes:** PK.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| track_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `tracks(id)` |
| depth | smallint | no | — | PK part; `CHECK (depth BETWEEN 1 AND 10)` |
| level_code | text | no | — | e.g. `subject`, `chapter`, `topic`; uq `(track_id, level_code)` |
| name_bn | text | no | — | |
| name_en | text | no | — | |

#### `curriculum.syllabus_versions`
- **Why:** One syllabus per track per year (TAX-03). A question can map to nodes in several versions.
- **Owner:** TAX.
- **Features:** TAX-03.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `tracks`; 1–N to `taxonomy_nodes`.
- **Read/write:** Read-only in practice.
- **Indexes:** Unique `(track_id, year)`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| track_id | uuid | no | — | FK `tracks(id)` |
| year | smallint | no | — | `CHECK (year BETWEEN 2020 AND 2045)` |
| status | text | no | 'draft' | `CHECK (status IN ('draft','active','retired'))` |
| copied_from_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `syllabus_versions(id)`; copy-forward from the previous year |
| published_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

#### `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes`
- **Why:** The configurable-depth tree per syllabus version (TAX-01, TAX-04). It holds bilingual names, ordering, icons, a free-or-paid flag and a mark weight for readiness (ANL-01). `stable_key` identifies "the same chapter" across years.
- **Owner:** TAX.
- **Features:** TAX-01, TAX-03, TAX-04, PAY-02 (`is_free`), ANL-01.
- **Relations:**
  - N–1 to `syllabus_versions`.
  - Self-reference through `parent_id`.
  - Referenced by `question_node_map`, progress, blueprints, threads and products.
- **Read/write:**
  - Reads: the whole tree per syllabus version, cached.
  - Writes: subtree reads during selection (`path <@ :chapter_path`).
  - Size: about 15k nodes per syllabus version **[Assumption]**.
- **Indexes:**
  - Unique `(syllabus_version_id, stable_key)`.
  - GiST on `path`: subtree and ancestor queries.
  - `(parent_id, sort)`: children listing.
  - `(syllabus_version_id, level_code)`.
- **Risks:** Moving a subtree rewrites `path` for every descendant. Mitigation: moves are an admin operation done in one transaction, and the cache key is bumped.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| syllabus_version_id | uuid | no | — | FK `syllabus_versions(id)` |
| parent_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `taxonomy_nodes(id)`; null for roots |
| level_code | text | no | — | must exist in `track_levels` for the track (checked in the service) |
| depth | smallint | no | — | `CHECK (depth BETWEEN 1 AND 10)` |
| path | ltree | no | — | labels are short ids, e.g. `n01h2.n9ka1` |
| stable_key | text | no | — | `CHECK (stable_key ~ '^[a-z0-9_.-]+$')` |
| name_bn | text | no | — | |
| name_en | text | no | — | |
| sort | integer | no | 0 | |
| icon | text | yes | — | |
| is_free | boolean | no | false | |
| marks_weight | numeric(5,2) | yes | — | share of marks in the exam blueprint |
| is_active | boolean | no | true | |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `curriculum.track_exam_dates`
- **Why:** The exam start date per track and year. Used by the countdown (PLAN-01) and by "valid until exam date" products (PAY-01).
- **Owner:** TAX.
- **Features:** PLAN-01, PAY-01, ANL-08.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `tracks`.
- **Read/write:** Cached.
- **Indexes:** Unique `(track_id, exam_year)`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| track_id | uuid | no | — | FK `tracks(id)` |
| exam_year | smallint | no | — | |
| starts_on | date | no | — | |
| source_url | text | yes | — | |
| is_confirmed | boolean | no | false | |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `curriculum.boards`
- **Why:** Education boards (general, madrasah, technical), used by paper sources, profiles and filters.
- **Owner:** TAX.
- **Features:** QB-03, QB-09, AUTH-04.
- **Relations:** Referenced by `profiles.board_code`, `papers.board_code` and `question_versions.source_board`.
