### 4.7 `qbank`

QB owns this schema. Two rules matter most:

- **Published versions are immutable (QB-06).** An edit creates a new version.
- **Attempts pin a version id.** So the hot read, "give me these versions with their content", is cached by `question_version_id` in Redis with no invalidation.

Other modules read through `qb.get_versions(ids, lang)`, `qb.select_candidates(filter)` and `qb.get_answer_keys(ids)`. The last of these is restricted to PRAC, EXAM and AI.

#### `qbank.questions`
- **Why:** The identity of a question across versions. Holds the type, the tenant (private banks in B8), the family (B7) and the content rights (C22).
- **Owner:** QB.
- **Features:** QB-01, QB-01a, QB-01b, QB-06, QB-10, C22.
- **Relations:**
  - 1–N to `question_versions`.
  - N–N to `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes` through `question_node_map`.
  - N–1 to `question_families` (B7).
  - Referenced by bookmarks, notes, notebook, error reports, threads.
- **Read/write:**
  - Writes: bulk imports of up to about 150k.
  - Reads: through versions, by the selection queries.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(status, tenant_id)`.
  - `(family_id)`.
  - `(type)`.
  - `(source_rights) WHERE source_rights = 'unknown'`: the rights backlog.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.
- **Rule:** `source_rights = 'unknown'` blocks publication. This is enforced by `CHECK` together with the status values, as shown below.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| type | text | no | — | `CHECK (type IN ('mcq_single','mcq_multi_statement','mcq_stimulus','cq','short_answer','essay','fill_blank','true_false','matching','numeric'))` |
| tenant_id | uuid | yes | — | null means platform content |
| family_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `question_families(id)` (B7) |
| status | text | no | 'draft' | `CHECK (status IN ('draft','in_review','verified','published','retired'))` |
| source_rights | text | no | 'unknown' | `CHECK (source_rights IN ('own','licensed','public_board','unknown'))`; `CHECK (NOT (status = 'published' AND source_rights = 'unknown'))` |
| rights_note | text | yes | — | |
| current_version_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `question_versions(id)` DEFERRABLE |
| created_by | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `qbank.question_versions`
- **Why:** The immutable, published unit that every attempt pins (QB-06). Holds the key, marks, metadata and source (QB-03, QB-04).
- **Owner:** QB.
- **Features:** QB-03, QB-04, QB-06, CMS-02, CMS-04a.
- **Relations:**
  - N–1 to `questions`.
  - Self-reference through `supersedes_id`.
  - 1–2 to `qv_content` (bn, en).
  - 1–N to `cq_subparts` and `question_media`.
  - Referenced by attempts, papers, exams, citations and the canonical cache.
- **Read/write:**
  - Reads: session start loads 6–10 versions. Stage 4 is about 1,000 sessions/s, but the cache hit rate is above 99% because the rows are immutable.
  - Writes: on author edits and on publish.
- **Indexes:**
  - Unique `(question_id, version_no)`.
  - Partial `(question_id) WHERE status = 'published'`: current version.
  - `(status, render_status, index_status)`: the "selectable" gate.
  - GIN `tags`.
  - `(source_board, source_year)`.
- **Risks:**
  - `answer_key` leakage. Mitigation: never selected by learner payload queries. The repository has separate functions, `payload_columns` and `key_columns`.
- **Build:** 2.
- **Immutability:** a trigger rejects `UPDATE` of content columns when `status = 'published'`. Only `status`, `retired_at`, `render_status` and `index_status` may change. This defends in depth alongside the service rule.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| question_id | uuid | no | — | FK `questions(id)` |
| version_no | integer | no | — | `CHECK (version_no >= 1)` |
| answer_key | jsonb | no | — | validated per type in code (e.g. `{"correct":["ga"]}`, `{"value":3.5,"tolerance":0.01}`) |
| marks | numeric(7,2) | no | 1 | `CHECK (marks >= 0)` |
| time_estimate_s | integer | no | 60 | `CHECK (time_estimate_s BETWEEN 5 AND 7200)` |
| difficulty_author | smallint | yes | — | `CHECK (difficulty_author BETWEEN 1 AND 5)` |
| difficulty_computed | numeric(4,3) | yes | — | p-value (QB-11) |
| bloom_level | text | yes | — | `CHECK (bloom_level IN ('knowledge','comprehension','application','higher_ability'))` |
| source | jsonb | no | '{}' | `{board, year, school, university, unit, bcs_no, exam_name}` |
| source_board | text | yes | — | FK `curriculum.boards(code)` |
| source_year | smallint | yes | — | |
| source_kind | text | yes | — | `CHECK (source_kind IN ('board','admission','job','school','textbook_exercise','model_test','original'))` |
| tags | text[] | no | '{}' | |
| video_url | text | yes | — | `CHECK (video_url ~ '^https://')` |
| concept_note_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `concept_notes(id)` |
| status | text | no | 'draft' | `CHECK (status IN ('draft','in_review','verified','published','retired'))` |
| supersedes_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `question_versions(id)` |
| authored_by | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| verified_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| published_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| retired_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| render_status | text | no | 'pending' | `CHECK (render_status IN ('pending','done','failed'))` |
| index_status | text | no | 'pending' | `CHECK (index_status IN ('pending','done','failed'))` |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `qbank.qv_content`
- **Why:** The Bangla and English content of one version. Both languages share one key and one identity (QB-05). Rendered HTML is produced at publish (KaTeX on the server, v2 §0.3).
- **Owner:** QB.
- **Features:** QB-02, QB-05, NFR-05.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `question_versions`, at most 2 rows per version.
- **Read/write:** Read with the versions; cached.
- **Indexes:** PK.
- **Risks:** Large rows (tables, SVG). Mitigations: media lives in `question_media`, and TOAST compression is used.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| question_version_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `question_versions(id)` |
| lang | text | no | — | PK part; `CHECK (lang IN ('bn','en'))` |
| body | text | no | — | Unicode + LaTeX source; NFC-normalised |
| options | jsonb | yes | — | `[{label, text}]` for objective types |
| stimulus | text | yes | — | for stimulus and situational MCQ |
| explanation | text | yes | — | |
| rendered_html | text | yes | — | |
| rendered_options | jsonb | yes | — | |
| rendered_explanation | text | yes | — | |
| content_sha256 | bytea | no | — | |

#### `qbank.cq_subparts`
- **Why:** The parts of a CQ (ক, খ, গ, ঘ). The number of parts and their marks are data: higher maths and accounting use 3 parts (market validation §2.1).
- **Owner:** QB.
- **Features:** QB-01a, AI-07, AI-07a.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `question_versions`; 1–N to `rubric_points`.
- **Read/write:** Read with the versions and by the marking job.
- **Indexes:** Unique `(question_version_id, ordinal)`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| question_version_id | uuid | no | — | FK `question_versions(id)` |
| label | text | no | — | e.g. `ক` |
| ordinal | smallint | no | — | `CHECK (ordinal BETWEEN 1 AND 8)` |
| marks | numeric(7,2) | no | — | `CHECK (marks > 0)` |
| bloom_level | text | yes | — | same list as `question_versions.bloom_level` |
| prompt_bn | text | no | — | |
| prompt_en | text | yes | — | |
| model_answer_bn | text | yes | — | |
| model_answer_en | text | yes | — | |

#### `qbank.rubric_points`
- **Why:** The rubric points per part, written by a subject teacher. No official marking scheme exists.
- **Owner:** QB.
- **Features:** QB-01a, AI-07a, AI-07b.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `cq_subparts`. Referenced by `ai.grading_parts`.
- **Read/write:** Read by marking and by the teacher review screen.
- **Indexes:** `(subpart_id, ordinal)` unique.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| subpart_id | uuid | no | — | FK `cq_subparts(id)` |
| ordinal | smallint | no | — | |
| description_bn | text | no | — | |
| description_en | text | yes | — | |
| marks | numeric(7,2) | no | — | `CHECK (marks > 0)` |
| authored_by | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |

#### `qbank.question_node_map`
- **Why:** Maps a question to taxonomy nodes across several syllabus versions (TAX-03). Mapping is by question, not by version, so a correction keeps its mapping.
- **Owner:** QB.
- **Features:** TAX-03, QB-03, PRAC-01, PRAC-03.
- **Relations:** N–N between `questions` and `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes`.
- **Read/write:** Read by session composition ("published questions under a topic"). Stage 4: about 1,000 reads/s, served from the candidate cache per node.
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `(question_id, node_id)`.
  - `(node_id, question_id)`: selection by node.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| question_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `questions(id)` |
| node_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes(id)` |
| is_primary | boolean | no | false | one primary node per syllabus version (service rule) |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `qbank.question_media`
- **Why:** Images, SVG diagrams and audio for a version (QB-02). Lite mode (OFF-02) returns references only. Alt text supports NFR-11.
- **Owner:** QB.
- **Features:** QB-02, OFF-02, NFR-11, NFR-06a.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `question_versions`.
- **Read/write:** Read with the payload. Bytes are served through short-lived signed CDN URLs.
- **Indexes:** `(question_version_id)`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| question_version_id | uuid | no | — | FK `question_versions(id)` |
| kind | text | no | — | `CHECK (kind IN ('image','svg','audio'))` |
| storage_key | text | no | — | private bucket key |
| mime | text | no | — | `CHECK (mime IN ('image/png','image/jpeg','image/webp','image/svg+xml','audio/mpeg','audio/ogg'))` |
| bytes | integer | no | — | `CHECK (bytes BETWEEN 1 AND 5242880)` |
| width | integer | yes | — | |
| height | integer | yes | — | |
| alt_text_bn | text | yes | — | |
| alt_text_en | text | yes | — | |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `qbank.import_jobs`

| default_node_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes(id)` |
| status | text | no | 'uploaded' | `CHECK (status IN ('uploaded','validating','validated','committing','committed','failed'))` |

| report_key | text | yes | — | downloadable validation report |

| finished_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

#### `qbank.import_rows`
- **Why:** Row-level validation results. This is the "validation report" of QB-07.
- **Owner:** QB.
- **Features:** QB-07.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `import_jobs`. Optionally a soft link to the created `questions` row.
- **Read/write:** Bulk inserts of up to 50k rows per job.
- **Indexes:** Unique `(import_job_id, row_no)`; `(import_job_id, status)`.
- **Risks:** Volume per job. Mitigation: rows are deleted 90 days after commit, and the report file is kept.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| import_job_id | uuid | no | — | FK `import_jobs(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE |
| row_no | integer | no | — | |
| status | text | no | — | `CHECK (status IN ('valid','invalid','imported','duplicate','skipped'))` |
| errors | jsonb | no | '[]' | |
| question_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `questions(id)` |
| payload | jsonb | no | — | parsed row |

### 4.8 `cms`

CMS owns this schema. CMS changes `qbank` only by calling QB functions (`qb.transition_status`, `qb.create_version`, `qb.publish`). It never writes `qbank` tables directly.

#### `cms.workflow_events`
- **Why:** Status transition history: Draft, then In Review, Verified, Published and Retired (CMS-02). Also the per-question audit trail.
- **Owner:** CMS.
- **Features:** CMS-02, CMS-04a.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `qbank.questions` and `qbank.question_versions`.
- **Read/write:** One row per transition.
- **Indexes:** `(question_id, created_at)`; `(actor_id, created_at DESC)`, used by productivity reports (CMS-09).
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| question_id | uuid | no | — | FK `qbank.questions(id)` |
| question_version_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `qbank.question_versions(id)` |
| from_status | text | yes | — | same status list as `qbank.questions` |
| to_status | text | no | — | same status list |
| actor_id | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| note | text | yes | — | |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `cms.key_verifications`
- **Why:** Blind two-person key verification (CMS-02). The verifier enters the key without seeing the author's. A match sets the status to Verified; a mismatch sends the item to a tiebreak. The database rejects verifier = author.
- **Owner:** CMS.
- **Features:** CMS-02.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `qbank.question_versions`.
- **Read/write:** One or two rows per version.
- **Indexes:** Unique `(question_version_id, verifier_id)`; `(question_version_id, role)`.
- **Risks:** Two verifiers racing (R7). Mitigation: a lock on the version row.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| question_version_id | uuid | no | — | FK `qbank.question_versions(id)` |
| author_id | uuid | no | — | copied from QB at insert; FK `identity.users(id)` |
| verifier_id | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)`; `CHECK (verifier_id <> author_id)` |
| role | text | no | 'verifier' | `CHECK (role IN ('verifier','tiebreak'))` |
| entered_key | jsonb | no | — | |
| matches | boolean | no | — | |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `cms.review_assignments`
- **Why:** Assignment queues per subject for authors and reviewers (CMS-03). They cover review, verify, tiebreak, error triage and AI review, each with an SLA.
- **Owner:** CMS.
- **Features:** CMS-03, CMS-02, CMS-04, AI-05a.
- **Relations:**
  - N–1 to `identity.users` (the assignee).
  - Soft references to `qbank.questions` and to `cms.error_reports` or `cms.ai_review_items`.
- **Read/write:** Queue reads by each reviewer.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(assignee_id, status, due_at)`: my queue.
  - `(subject_node_id, status, due_at)`: unassigned pool.
  - Unique partial `(question_id, kind) WHERE status IN ('open','in_progress')`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| question_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `qbank.questions(id)` |
| item_ref_id | uuid | yes | — | error report or AI review item id |
| assignee_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `identity.users(id)`; null means pooled |
| kind | text | no | — | `CHECK (kind IN ('review','verify','tiebreak','error_triage','ai_review'))` |
| subject_node_id | uuid | no | — | FK `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes(id)` |
| status | text | no | 'open' | `CHECK (status IN ('open','in_progress','done','escalated','cancelled'))` |
| due_at | timestamptz | no | — | |
| assigned_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| completed_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

#### `cms.editor_drafts`
- **Why:** Autosaved editor state, so edits survive a closed tab (CMS-01, v2 §7.1 step 4).
- **Owner:** CMS.
- **Features:** CMS-01.
- **Relations:** N–1 to `qbank.questions` and to `identity.users`.
- **Read/write:** Autosave every 10 s while editing.
- **Indexes:** Unique `(question_id, editor_id)`.
- **Risks:** None.
- **Build:** 2.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| question_id | uuid | no | — | FK `qbank.questions(id)` |
| editor_id | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| base_version_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `qbank.question_versions(id)`; detects conflicting edits |
| payload | jsonb | no | — | |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `cms.error_reports`
- **Why:** The student error-report queue (PRAC-07, CMS-04). Reports on one question are merged. The SLA is triage in 24 h and resolution in 72 h (v2 §7.1).
- **Owner:** CMS.
- **Features:** PRAC-07, CMS-04, CMS-04a.
- **Relations:**
  - N–1 to `qbank.questions` and `qbank.question_versions`.
  - Self-reference through `merged_into_id`.
  - 1–0..1 to `corrections`.
- **Read/write:** Stage 4: about 2 reports per minute.
- **Indexes:**
  - `(status, triage_due_at)`: SLA queue.
  - `(question_id, status)`: merge.
  - `(reporter_user_id, created_at DESC)`: per-user limit.
- **Risks:** Spam. Mitigation: at most 20 reports per user per day, plus moderated text.
- **Build:** 2. The student endpoint is in 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| question_id | uuid | no | — | FK `qbank.questions(id)` |
| question_version_id | uuid | no | — | FK `qbank.question_versions(id)` |
| reporter_user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| kind | text | no | — | `CHECK (kind IN ('wrong_key','typo','wrong_explanation','out_of_syllabus','other'))` |
| text | text | yes | — | `CHECK (char_length(text) <= 1000)`; moderated |
| status | text | no | 'open' | `CHECK (status IN ('open','triaged','resolved','rejected','merged'))` |
| merged_into_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `error_reports(id)`; `CHECK ((status='merged') = (merged_into_id IS NOT NULL))` |
| triage_due_at | timestamptz | no | now() + interval '24 hours' | |
| resolve_due_at | timestamptz | no | now() + interval '72 hours' | |
| resolution | text | yes | — | `CHECK (resolution IN ('key_corrected','content_fixed','no_change','retired'))` |
| resolved_by | uuid | yes | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| resolved_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |

#### `cms.corrections`
- **Why:** A key or content correction and the rescoring run it drives (CMS-04a, v2 §7.1 steps 9–11). Rescoring is resumable from the cursors. If a published merit list changes, ops must approve its republication.
- **Owner:** CMS.
- **Features:** CMS-04a, EXAM-06.
- **Relations:**
  - N–1 to `qbank.questions`.
  - Old and new N–1 links to `qbank.question_versions`.
  - N–0..1 to `error_reports`.
  - Referenced by `practice.score_adjustments.correction_id`.
- **Read/write:** Rare. CMS creates the row and emits `correction.created`. The rescoring itself is the PRAC job `prac.rescore_correction`, which reports through the `rescoring.progress` and `rescoring.completed` events. CMS consumes them and updates `rescoring_status`, `affected_users` and the progress cursor mirror. CMS never writes practice data (R-03).
- **Indexes:** `(rescoring_status)`; unique `(old_version_id, new_version_id)`.
- **Risks:** A long rescoring run. Mitigation: throttled batches on the `rescoring` queue.
- **Build:** 3. Exam cursors and merit approval are added in 5.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| question_id | uuid | no | — | FK `qbank.questions(id)` |
| old_version_id | uuid | no | — | FK `qbank.question_versions(id)` |
| new_version_id | uuid | no | — | FK `qbank.question_versions(id)`; `CHECK (old_version_id <> new_version_id)` |
| error_report_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `error_reports(id)` |
| reason | text | no | — | |
| key_changed | boolean | no | — | rescoring runs only when true |
| rescoring_status | text | no | 'pending' | `CHECK (rescoring_status IN ('pending','running','practice_done','exam_done','done','failed','not_needed'))` |
| practice_cursor | jsonb | yes | — | `{partition, last_user_id, last_answer_id}` |
| exam_cursor | jsonb | yes | — | |
| affected_users | integer | no | 0 | |
| merit_republish_required | boolean | no | false | |
| merit_approved_by | uuid | yes | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| created_by | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| finished_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

#### `cms.ai_review_items`
- **Why:** The AI feedback queue (AI-05a, v2 §4.6). It collects thumbs-down ratings, failed verifications and disagreements with the answer key. The outcomes are: correct the cache, add an eval case, raise an error report, or change a prompt.
- **Owner:** CMS.
- **Features:** AI-05a, AI-15a.
- **Relations:**
  - Soft reference to `ai.tutor_messages`, which is partitioned and owned by AI.
  - N–1 to `qbank.question_versions`.
- **Read/write:** Stage 4: about 1/s at a 2% thumbs-down rate **[Assumption]**.
- **Indexes:** `(status, subject_node_id, created_at)`; `(question_version_id)`.
- **Risks:** Queue overload. Mitigation: dedupe per `(question_version_id, source)` while an item is open (unique partial).
- **Build:** 4.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| source | text | no | — | `CHECK (source IN ('thumbs_down','verify_fail','key_disagreement'))` |
| tutor_message_id | uuid | yes | — | soft ref |
| question_version_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `qbank.question_versions(id)` |
| subject_node_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes(id)` |
| summary | text | yes | — | |
| status | text | no | 'open' | `CHECK (status IN ('open','in_progress','resolved'))` |
| outcome | text | yes | — | `CHECK (outcome IN ('cache_corrected','eval_added','error_report','prompt_change','dismissed'))` |
| assignee_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | |
| resolved_at | timestamptz | yes | — | |

#### 4.A Accepted supporting columns (platform … cms)

§3 lists key columns only. The columns below were added in §4 as supporting columns, not new tables, and are **accepted** as part of this design.

| Item | Reason |
|---|---|
| `platform.outbox.relay_attempts` | Lets the relay back off on rows that keep failing |
| `platform.jobs.queue` value `campaign` | v2 §2.2: campaigns must not delay transactional pushes |
| `platform.dead_jobs.queue`, `resolution` | Replay tooling |
| `platform.relay_checkpoints.s3_object_key` | Debugging the Parquet flush |
| `platform.search_documents.question_type`, `difficulty`, `source_kind`, `source_year` | Learner and CMS filters (CMS-05) without joining `qbank` |
| `identity.external_identities.unlink_reason` | Meta deauthorise, data deletion and Apple revocation flows |
| `identity.otp_challenges.sms_message_id` | Links each OTP to its SMS cost record |
| `identity.device_sessions.user_agent`, `sign_in_method`, `created_at` | Session list display and anomaly signals |
| `identity.roles.is_staff`, `identity.permissions.module` | Admin-audience rule and ownership |
| `identity.consent_documents.kind` | One table for the consent, privacy and terms documents |
| `identity.institutions.source` | Track user-suggested institutions |
| `community.moderation_decisions.user_id`, `created_by` columns on terms | Per-user moderation history |
| `community.threads.forum_category_id`, `mentor_requests.text/subject_node_id` | Needed by COM-03 and COM-02 |
| `notify.notifications.is_critical`, `deliveries.push_token_id` | Quiet-hours bypass; token invalidation |
| `ops.audit_log.actor_kind` | System and provider actors (webhooks, jobs) |
| `ops.banners.created_by`, `notices.created_by`, `campaigns.created_by` | Ownership |
| `curriculum.syllabus_versions.copied_from_id` | Copy a syllabus forward to the next year |
| `qbank.question_versions.source_kind`, `qv_content.stimulus`, `cq_subparts.prompt_bn/en` | QB-01 stimulus MCQ; PRAC-03 textbook-exercise filter; CQ part prompts were missing in §3 |
| `qbank.import_jobs.source_note/default_node_id`, `import_pages.ocr_engine/cost_micro_usd` | Import UX and OCR cost tracking |
| `cms.review_assignments.item_ref_id`, `cms.editor_drafts.base_version_id`, `cms.corrections.key_changed/created_by/finished_at`, `cms.ai_review_items.summary`, `cms.ai_drafts.requested_by` | Queue linkage, edit-conflict detection, rescoring control |

Notes:

- §3.1 says the HNSW index on `search_documents` comes in B4. B2 needs the `pgvector` extension only if the column is created then, so the plan is: create the column in B4 and create the extension in B1.
- `community.threads.question_id` FK points to `qbank.questions`, which §1.1 allows.
- `notify.notifications` dedupe relies on `processed_events`, not on a unique index (partition-key constraint), as §3.4 implies.

### 4.9 `practice`

Rates use the stages in §5.11: Stage 1 = 1,000 concurrent learners, Stage 4 = 100,000 concurrent learners. **[Assumption]**

#### `practice.practice_sessions`

- **Why:** A practice session pins an ordered set of question versions. Every entry point (lesson, custom builder, chapter, textbook exercise, past paper, mistakes, weak topic, similar questions, and later Daily Mix, diagnostic and speed drill) produces the same record, so the learning loop has one shape (v2 §1.4).
- **Owner:** PRAC.
- **Features:** PRAC-01, PRAC-02, PRAC-03, PRAC-04, PRAC-05 (mistake re-practice), ANL-03 (one-tap weak-topic practice), AI-03 ("Practise these"); in Build 7, PRAC-10, PRAC-11, PRAC-12.
- **Relations:**
  - `identity.users` 1—N sessions.
  - `identity.track_enrolments` 1—N sessions.
  - Sessions 1—N `session_items`.
  - Sessions 1—N `attempt_answers` (soft reference, no FK).
  - `qbank.papers` 0..1—N sessions.
  - `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes` 0..1—N sessions.
- **Read/write pattern:**
  - Insert at session start: about 10/s at Stage 1, about 1,000/s at Stage 4.
  - Update on completion at a similar rate.
  - Read by id on resume, and through the recent-sessions list.
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `id`.
  - uq `(user_id, client_session_id)`: replay-safe session creation.
  - `ix_practice_sessions_user_started (user_id, started_at DESC)`: history and resume.
  - Partial `(user_id) WHERE status='open'`: resume the open session on the home screen.
- **Scalability risks:** Grows about 1 row per 6 answers, and is never partitioned. At Stage 4 that is about 5M rows a day. Mitigation: about 120 B per row; if the table passes 2B rows, abandoned sessions older than 90 days are archived to S3 and deleted. Completed sessions are kept forever because attempts reference them.
- **Build:** 3. Build 7 adds `kind` values (`daily_mix`, `diagnostic`, `speed_drill`) by expanding the CHECK.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK (UUIDv7, server-generated) |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` RESTRICT |
| track_enrolment_id | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.track_enrolments(id)` RESTRICT |
| client_session_id | uuid | no | — | uq with `user_id` (device-generated) |
| mode | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('learn','test') |
| kind | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('lesson','custom','chapter','textbook','past_paper','mistakes','weak_topic','similar','review','ai_quiz','daily_mix','diagnostic','speed_drill'); all values listed from B3 so later builds need no constraint swap |
| filters | jsonb | no | '{}' | Builder filters (nodes, count, difficulty, source, type), versioned by the application |
| node_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes(id)` RESTRICT |
| paper_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `qbank.papers(id)` RESTRICT |
| time_limit_s | integer | yes | — | CHECK (time_limit_s BETWEEN 60 AND 14400) |
| negative_marking | numeric(4,2) | no | 0 | CHECK (negative_marking >= 0 AND negative_marking <= 1) |
| status | text | no | 'open' | CHECK IN ('open','completed','abandoned') |
| item_count | smallint | no | — | CHECK (item_count BETWEEN 1 AND 200) |
| started_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |
| completed_at | timestamptz | yes | — | CHECK (status <> 'completed' OR completed_at IS NOT NULL) |
| summary | jsonb | yes | — | Score, XP earned, streak, next recommendation, written at completion |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

#### `practice.session_items`

- **Why:** The exact versions served in a session, in order. A resume or a Test-mode result always shows the same items, even after a correction publishes a new version (QB-06).
- **Owner:** PRAC.
- **Features:** PRAC-01–04, PRAC-02 (Test-mode reveal at the end).
- **Relations:**
  - `practice_sessions` 1—N items (FK CASCADE, because a session that is never started can be deleted in tests).
  - `qbank.question_versions` 1—N items (FK RESTRICT).
- **Read/write pattern:**
  - Bulk insert of 5–30 rows per session start: about 80 rows/s at Stage 1, about 8,000 rows/s at Stage 4.
  - Read by `session_id` on resume and on completion.
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `(session_id, position)`: ordered fetch.
  - `ix (question_version_id)` is **not** created; rescoring finds answers through `attempt_answers`, not through items.
- **Scalability risks:** This is the second-largest PRAC table, at about 8 items per session. Mitigation: narrow rows (about 60 B) and no secondary index.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| session_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `practice_sessions(id)` CASCADE |
| position | smallint | no | — | PK part; CHECK (position >= 1) |
| question_id | uuid | no | — | FK `qbank.questions(id)` RESTRICT |
| question_version_id | uuid | no | — | FK `qbank.question_versions(id)` RESTRICT |

#### `practice.attempt_answers` (partitioned)

- **Why:** The single source of truth for every scored answer in every mode: practice Learn and Test, exam attempts copied in after final scoring, re-attempts, duels, and self-marked written answers. The Mistake Notebook, topic progress, readiness, mastery, XP and item statistics are all derived from it (v2 §1 and §3.6).
- **Owner:** PRAC.
- **Features:** PRAC-02, PRAC-05, ANL-01, ANL-03, GAM-01, GAM-02, CMS-04a, NFR-09, QB-11 (through the event log), EXAM-08, PRAC-13 (Build 9).
- **Relations:** all are soft references, with **no outgoing FKs** (§1.1 exception, R-14). The application validates them at write time.
  - `identity.users` 1—N.
  - `identity.track_enrolments` 1—N.
  - `practice_sessions` 0..1—N, through a soft reference `session_id`.
  - `exam.exam_attempts` 0..1—N, through a soft reference `exam_attempt_id`.
  - `qbank.question_versions` 1—N (validated through `qb.get_versions()`).
  - 1—N `score_adjustments`, by `(user_id, answer_id)`.
  - The source of `rewards.reward_transactions`, through `source_id`.
- **Partitioning:**
  - `PARTITION BY HASH (user_id)`, **64 partitions** `attempt_answers_p00` … `p63`, created in the Build 3 migration by the helper.
  - At Stage 4 (about 30M rows/day, about 11B a year) that is about 170M rows per partition per year. Changing the modulus later needs a new table and a backfill, so it is set high now.
- **Read/write pattern:**
  - Answer insert: rates from [`17`](17_scalability_and_performance.md) §2.2, about 17/s at Stage 1 and about 1,700/s sustained (about 2,500 peak) at Stage 4.
  - Exam copy-in after the final close: about 20M rows per 200k-candidate exam, in jobs of 1,000 attempts (about 5k rows/s, throttled).
  - Reads:
    - Notebook recompute: `(user_id, question_id)`, about 1 per answer.
    - The 24-hour repeat check: `(user_id, question_id, effective_at)`.
    - Session result: `(user_id, session_id)`.
    - Rescoring: `question_version_id`, in rare large scans.
- **`effective_at` rules (v2 §5.3):**
  - `effective_at` = the device's `last_server_sync` + (`monotonic_since_sync_ms` − the monotonic value at sync).
  - It is accepted only if `received_at − effective_at ≤ 6 h` (P0; 72 h when OFF-03 packs ship in Build 7) and `effective_at ≤ received_at + 2 min` (no future answers).
  - Otherwise, or when the client has no monotonic evidence (a reboot or a web reload), `effective_at = received_at`.
  - `effective_source` records which rule applied. `effective_dhaka_date = (effective_at AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Dhaka')::date`, computed in the application.
  - `answered_at` stores the device's wall-clock claim, for audit only. It is never used for rules.
- **`flags` bitmask (smallint):**

  | Bit | Meaning |
  |---|---|
  | 1 | `rapid_guess`: under 15% of `time_estimate_s`, with a minimum of 3 s |
  | 2 | `late_sync`: outside the tolerance window, so the date fell back to receipt |
  | 4 | `retired_version`: answered against a version retired before receipt |
  | 8 | `ai_item`: from an AI quiz (Build 7); completion XP only |
  | 16 | `self_marked` |
  | 32 | `duplicate_attempt_suppressed`: the same question within 24 h, so 0 XP (informational) |
- **Indexes (local to each partition):**
  - PK `(user_id, id)`: idempotency (C4), `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`.
  - `ix_attempt_answers_user_question_eff (user_id, question_id, effective_at DESC)`: notebook recompute and the 24 h repeat rule.
  - `ix_attempt_answers_qv (question_version_id)`: rescoring by corrected version (CMS-04a).
  - `ix_attempt_answers_user_session (user_id, session_id) WHERE session_id IS NOT NULL`: session summary.
  - `ix_attempt_answers_exam_attempt (exam_attempt_id) WHERE exam_attempt_id IS NOT NULL`: re-scoring copied exam answers.
  - BRIN `(received_at)`: time-ranged repair and replay jobs.
- **Scalability risks:**
  - About 1 KB per row including indexes: about 0.7 TB in year 1, and about 10 TB a year at Stage 4.
  - Mitigations:
    - The hash partitions keep each B-tree manageable and let vacuum run per partition.
    - Rows are immutable once `is_correct` is set. The only `UPDATE` is the one-time self-mark of a written answer (`UPDATE … WHERE is_correct IS NULL`), so update bloat is negligible.
    - At Stage 4 the `practice` and `rewards` schemas can move together to their own cluster (§5.9); having no outgoing FKs makes that possible.
  - The `question_version_id` index is the largest secondary index. Rescoring a popular version scans all 64 partitions; that is acceptable because it is a rare batch job.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK part (device-generated UUIDv7) |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; partition key; soft ref `identity.users` (no FK, §1.1) |
| track_enrolment_id | uuid | no | — | soft ref `identity.track_enrolments` (no FK) |
| session_id | uuid | yes | — | soft ref `practice_sessions.id` |
| exam_attempt_id | uuid | yes | — | soft ref `exam.exam_attempts.id`; CHECK (num_nonnulls(session_id, exam_attempt_id) <= 1) |
| context | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('practice_learn','practice_test','written_self','exam_live','exam_practice','exam_reattempt','duel') (canonical list, R-32) |
| question_id | uuid | no | — | soft ref `qbank.questions` (no FK) |
| question_version_id | uuid | no | — | soft ref `qbank.question_versions` (no FK; validated by `qb.get_versions()`) |
| lang | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('bn','en') |
| response | jsonb | no | — | Validated per question type by the application |
| is_correct | boolean | yes | — | NULL for skipped answers and for written self-marked answers until the self-mark; set exactly once (`WHERE is_correct IS NULL`) and immutable afterwards |
| marks_awarded | numeric(7,2) | no | 0 | — |
| time_spent_ms | integer | no | 0 | CHECK (time_spent_ms BETWEEN 0 AND 86400000) |
| answered_at | timestamptz | no | — | Device wall clock (audit only) |
| received_at | timestamptz | no | now() | Server receipt time |
| effective_at | timestamptz | no | — | Rules above |
| effective_dhaka_date | date | no | — | — |
| effective_source | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('device','receipt') |
| scoring_source | text | no | 'auto' | CHECK IN ('auto','self','teacher','ai_estimate') |
| flags | smallint | no | 0 | CHECK (flags >= 0) |

#### `practice.score_adjustments`

- **Why:** Answers are immutable once scored (`is_correct` set). A key correction writes an adjustment row, and projections read the answer together with its latest adjustment (v2 §3.1, §5.5).
- **Owner:** PRAC.
- **Features:** CMS-04a; EXAM-07 (rescored exam answers copied into practice records).
- **Relations:**
  - `attempt_answers` 1—N, through `(user_id, answer_id)`, a soft reference into the partitioned table.
  - `cms.corrections` 1—N (soft reference; another module's table).
- **Read/write pattern:**
  - Bursty inserts during rescoring: up to about 5,000/s, throttled in batches.
  - Read in every notebook recompute for that user and question.
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `id`.
  - uq `(user_id, answer_id, correction_id)`: makes rescoring idempotent.
  - `ix (user_id, answer_id, created_at DESC)`: the latest adjustment per answer during a recompute.
- **Scalability risks:** Corrections are rare, so the table stays small (millions of rows at most). It is not partitioned.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` RESTRICT |
| answer_id | uuid | no | — | soft ref `attempt_answers(user_id, id)` |
| correction_id | uuid | no | — | soft ref `cms.corrections.id` |
| old_is_correct | boolean | yes | — | — |
| new_is_correct | boolean | yes | — | — |
| old_marks | numeric(7,2) | no | — | — |
| new_marks | numeric(7,2) | no | — | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | uq `(user_id, answer_id, correction_id)` |

#### `practice.mistake_entries`

- **Why:** The Mistake Notebook projection (PRAC-05). It is recomputed from the user's rescored answers on that question, in `effective_at` order, never kept as a counter. Replays, out-of-order syncs and corrections therefore cannot corrupt it (v2 §1.2).
- **Owner:** PRAC.
- **Features:** PRAC-05, the v2 §1.1 lesson composition (one notebook item per lesson), the "tomorrow" card, PLAN reminders ("Review 3 mistakes").
- **Relations:**
  - `identity.users` 1—N.
  - `qbank.questions` 1—N, keyed by question, not version, so a correction carries over.
  - `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes` (subject) 1—N.
- **Recompute algorithm** (inside the answer transaction):
  1. `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` on `(user_id, question_id)`, then `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` (R2).
  2. Read the user's answers for this question with their latest adjustment, ordered by `effective_at`, then `received_at`, then `id`.
  3. Let `effective_correct` be the adjusted value if one exists, otherwise `is_correct`. Treat skipped answers as wrong.
  4. **Open** if any wrong or skipped answer exists and the two most recent answers are not both correct. **Cleared** when the two most recent are correct; set `cleared_at` to the effective time of the second.
  5. `consecutive_correct` = the count of trailing correct answers.
  6. A cleared entry reopens when a later wrong answer arrives.
  7. A transition from open to cleared emits the reward `xp:mistake_cleared:{question_id}`, once per question for life.
- **Read/write pattern:**
  - Upsert on every answer: about 25/s at Stage 1, about 2,500/s at Stage 4.
  - Read per user (notebook list, lesson composer).
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `(user_id, question_id)`: recompute lock and lookup.
  - `ix_mistake_entries_user_open (user_id, subject_node_id, last_answer_at DESC) WHERE status='open'`: the notebook list and the lesson composer.
- **Scalability risks:**
  - The read in step 2 is bounded by the user's attempts on one question (usually fewer than 10).
  - The table can hold every question a user has ever answered (≤ 100k rows per user, usually hundreds). Rows with no wrong answers are never inserted: step 1 runs only when the incoming answer is wrong, skipped or adjusted, or when a row already exists.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `identity.users(id)` RESTRICT |
| question_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `qbank.questions(id)` RESTRICT |
| subject_node_id | uuid | no | — | FK `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes(id)` RESTRICT |
| status | text | no | 'open' | CHECK IN ('open','cleared') |
| first_wrong_at | timestamptz | no | — | — |
| last_answer_at | timestamptz | no | — | — |
| consecutive_correct | smallint | no | 0 | CHECK (consecutive_correct >= 0) |
| cleared_at | timestamptz | yes | — | CHECK ((status='cleared') = (cleared_at IS NOT NULL)) |
| projection_version | smallint | no | 1 | Matches `analytics.projection_versions` |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

- **Why:** User-named folders for bookmarks (PRAC-06).
- **Owner:** PRAC.
- **Features:** PRAC-06.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—N folders; a folder 1—N `bookmarks` (FK SET NULL, so deleting a folder keeps its bookmarks unfiled).
- **Read/write pattern:** Rare writes. Read with the bookmarks list.
- **Indexes:** PK `id`; uq `(user_id, name)`.

#### `practice.question_notes`

- **Why:** A personal note on any question (PRAC-06).
- **Owner:** PRAC.
- **Features:** PRAC-06.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—N; `qbank.questions` 1—N.
- **Read/write pattern:** Upsert on save. Read with the session payload: `qb` supplies the question, then PRAC attaches the note through a batched PK read.
- **Indexes:** PK `(user_id, question_id)`.

#### `practice.ability_estimates`

- **Why:** The per-topic, Elo-style ability that drives the adaptive `NextQuestionPolicy` (60–75% predicted success) and seeds mastery.
- **Owner:** PRAC.
- **Features:** PRAC-08, PRAC-12, ANL-04.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—N; `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes` (topic) 1—N.
- **Read/write pattern:**
  - Updated by the `prac.ability.on_answer` consumer of `answer.recorded`: about 25/s at Stage 1, about 2,500/s at Stage 4. This is asynchronous and is not in the answer transaction.
  - Read at session composition.
- **Indexes:** PK `(user_id, node_id)`.
- **Scalability risks:** Write volume equals the answer rate. The consumer batches updates per user within a 1 s window. The table is rebuildable from `attempt_answers` and item difficulty.

#### `practice.review_cards`

- **Why:** FSRS scheduling state for spaced repetition of mistakes and flashcards.
- **Owner:** PRAC.
- **Features:** PRAC-09, QB-13, PRAC-10 (due reviews make up 40% of the Daily Mix).
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—N; `qbank.questions` 0..1—N; `qbank.flashcards` 0..1—N.
- **Read/write pattern:** Created when a mistake opens or a deck is studied. Updated per review. The due-queue read is about 1 per session start.

  - uq `(user_id, card_type, question_id, flashcard_id) NULLS NOT DISTINCT`.
  - `ix_review_cards_due (user_id, due_at)`: the due queue.
- **Scalability risks:** Up to thousands of cards per user, but always queried by user.
- **Build:** 7.

#### `practice.offline_packs`

- **Why:** OFF-03 offline practice packs need a lifecycle (build, download, revoke, expire) tied to the entitlement that paid for the content.
- **Owner:** PRAC.
- **Features:** OFF-03, PAY-02 (entitlement-bound expiry), NFR-06a (accepted key-exposure risk, mitigated by watermarking and canaries).
- **Relations:** many packs → 1 `identity.users`; → 1 `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes` (chapter); soft reference to `commerce.entitlements` (validated through PAY).
- **Read/write pattern:** Stage 1 about 0.05 writes/s, Stage 4 about 5/s (download bursts before exams). It is read on sync and on the pack list.
- **Indexes:** `ix_offline_packs_user_status (user_id, status)` serves the pack list; `ix_offline_packs_expiry (expires_at) WHERE status='ready'` serves the expiry job.
- **Scalability risks:** Bundle generation is CPU-heavy, so it runs as `imports`-queue jobs, cached by `(chapter_node_id, content_version, lang)`; per-user wrapping is cheap. A limit of 5 packs per user per day.
- **Build:** 7.

### 4.10 `analytics`

#### `analytics.topic_progress`

- **Why:** Accuracy, attempts and time per taxonomy node, maintained inside the answer transaction. It feeds the dashboard, weak areas, readiness and the lesson path (ANL-01, ANL-03).
- **Owner:** ANL.
- **Features:** ANL-01, ANL-03, PRAC-01 ("tomorrow" card), PAR-01, INST-07.
- **Relations:**
  - `identity.users` 1—N.
  - `identity.track_enrolments` 1—N.
  - `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes` 1—N. One answer updates the topic row and each ancestor up to the subject (usually 3–4 rows).
- **Read/write pattern:**
  - Each answer upserts 3–4 rows (`INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE SET attempts = attempts + 1 …`): about 100 rows/s at Stage 1, about 10,000 rows/s at Stage 4.
  - The dashboard reads by PK prefix.
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `(user_id, track_enrolment_id, node_id)`: the dashboard tree and upserts.
  - `ix_topic_progress_weak (user_id, track_enrolment_id, attempts) WHERE attempts >= 10`: the weak-area scan (lowest accuracy among nodes with at least 10 attempts), computed in the service from this partial set.
- **Scalability risks:**
  - These are update-heavy rows: HOT updates are needed, so `fillfactor=80`, and no indexed column changes except `attempts`.
  - The weak-area partial index includes `attempts`, so each update that crosses 10 touches it. That is acceptable because it happens once per node.
  - Rebuildable from `attempt_answers` under a new `projection_version`.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `identity.users(id)` |
| track_enrolment_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `identity.track_enrolments(id)` |
| node_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `curriculum.taxonomy_nodes(id)` |
| attempts | integer | no | 0 | CHECK (attempts >= 0) |
| correct | integer | no | 0 | CHECK (correct <= attempts) |
| wrong | integer | no | 0 | — |
| skipped | integer | no | 0 | CHECK (correct + wrong + skipped = attempts) |
| time_spent_ms | bigint | no | 0 | — |
| last_attempt_at | timestamptz | yes | — | — |
| projection_version | smallint | no | 1 | — |

#### `analytics.daily_study`

- **Why:** Study time, answers and sessions per Dhaka day, for the dashboard and later the parent overview. It is deliberately separate from `rewards.daily_activity`: that table holds rule state for XP caps and streaks under the GAM rules, while this one holds display data that is rebuilt with ANL projections. The duplication is two integers per day, and it keeps each module's rebuild independent.
- **Owner:** ANL.
- **Features:** ANL-01, PAR-01, PAR-02, PAR-03 (daily time cap check), PAR-05.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—N; `identity.track_enrolments` 1—N.
- **Read/write pattern:** Upserted once per answer and once per session completion. Read as a 30–90 day range per user.
- **Indexes:** PK `(user_id, track_enrolment_id, dhaka_date)`, which serves the range read.
- **Scalability risks:** About 1M rows a day at Stage 4. Kept for 3 years, then aggregated to months.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| track_enrolment_id | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| dhaka_date | date | no | — | PK part |
| answers | integer | no | 0 | — |
| time_spent_ms | bigint | no | 0 | — |
| sessions | smallint | no | 0 | — |

- **Why:** Records which projection version readers use. A rebuild writes rows under a new version and then switches readers (v2 §3.6).
- **Owner:** ANL. It is also used by PRAC for `mistake_entries`, through the ANL public API.
- **Features:** NFR-09 rebuilds; release gate §8.2 (learning-record integrity).
- **Relations:** None (configuration).
- **Read/write pattern:** Read and cached at process start, and refreshed every 60 s.
- **Indexes:** PK `(projection, version)`; uq partial `(projection) WHERE status='active'`.
- **Scalability risks:** None.

| level | text | no | 'not_started' | CHECK IN ('not_started','learning','proficient','mastered') |
| score | numeric(5,2) | no | 0 | CHECK (score BETWEEN 0 AND 100) |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

#### `analytics.time_analytics`

- **Why:** Precomputed time-versus-accuracy metrics, rushed and slow patterns, guess detection and negative-mark loss, from nightly warehouse queries over the S3 log.
- **Owner:** ANL.
- **Features:** ANL-05.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—N.
- **Read/write pattern:** Nightly bulk upsert for active users. Read on demand.
- **Indexes:** PK `(user_id, track_enrolment_id, period_key)`.
- **Scalability risks:** The nightly job writes about 1M rows at Stage 4, through `COPY` into a staging table followed by a merge.
- **Build:** 7.

### 4.11 `rewards`

All rule numbers (XP values, caps, thresholds) live in `reward_rules.rules`, never in code or constraints (§4 rule 6 of `04`). The values below are v2 §5 rule version 1.

#### `rewards.reward_rules`

- **Why:** Versioned gamification rules (D6). Each ledger row records the `rule_version` it was awarded under, so a later rule change never rewrites history.
- **Owner:** GAM.
- **Features:** GAM-01, GAM-02, GAM-03, D6; GAM-04–06 rule data in Build 7.
- **Relations:** 1—N `reward_transactions` (soft reference by version).
- **Read/write pattern:** Published by staff, rarely. Cached in process (immutable per version). The active version is the highest `effective_from <= now()`.
- **Indexes:** PK `version`; uq `effective_from`.
- **Scalability risks:** None. Rules are validated against a pydantic schema before insert.
- **Build:** 3. Seed v1 (`03` §6).

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| version | integer | no | — | PK |
| effective_from | timestamptz | no | — | uq |
| rules | jsonb | no | — | Schema-validated; v1 = v2 §5.1–5.4 |
| created_by | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

#### `rewards.reward_transactions` (partitioned)

- **Why:** The append-only ledger of everything earned: XP, freeze grants and consumption, badge awards (B7) and coins (B9). Balances, levels and leaderboards are derived from it (v2 §3.1, §5.6).
- **Owner:** GAM.
- **Features:** GAM-01, GAM-02, GAM-03, GAM-04, GAM-06, GAM-08, CMS-04a (key-correction XP).
- **Relations:** all are soft references, with **no outgoing FKs** (§1.1 exception, R-14).
  - `identity.users` 1—N.
  - `identity.track_enrolments` 0..1—N (`NULL` for account-wide kinds such as freezes).
  - Sources, by `source_type` and `source_id`: attempt answers, sessions, exam attempts, mistake entries, Dhaka days, corrections, challenges.
  - Self-reference `reverses_id`. Reversals are used for admin fraud action and, from B7, to return a freeze when a provisional missed or frozen day becomes counted inside the 72 h offline window (S-9). v2 never reverses XP on a downward key correction.
- **Partitioning:** `HASH (user_id)`, **64 partitions**. The unique key `(user_id, idempotency_key)` contains the partition key.
- **Idempotency key formats (R-05).** These are exhaustive for rule v1, with the Build 6, 7 and 9 kinds included. They are built **only** by `app/modules/gam/contracts/reward_keys.py`; golden-value tests and a cross-path test (answer transaction, repair job, rebuild, exam consumer) assert identical keys ([`07`](07_build_03.md) §C.6.1).

  | reason_code | kind | amount (v1) | source | `idempotency_key` |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | `answer_attempted` | xp | 2 | answer | `xp:answer_attempted:{answer_id}` |
  | `answer_correct` | xp | 3 | answer | `xp:answer_correct:{answer_id}` |
  | `key_correction` | xp | 3 | answer + correction | `xp:key_correction:{answer_id}:{correction_id}` |
  | `mistake_cleared` | xp | 5 | question | `xp:mistake_cleared:{question_id}` (once per question for life) |
  | `session_completed` | xp | 10 | session (≥ 5 answers) | `xp:session_completed:{session_id}` |
  | `daily_goal` | xp | 10 | Dhaka day | `xp:daily_goal:{dhaka_date}` |
  | `mock_submitted` | xp | 20 | mock, model test or past-paper attempt | `xp:mock_submitted:{paper_or_exam_id}:{dhaka_date}` (first attempt of that paper per day) |
  | `live_exam_submitted` | xp | 30 | live exam attempt | `xp:live_exam_submitted:{attempt_id}` |
  | `written_attempt` (B6) | xp | attempt XP | teacher-batch written submission | `xp:written_attempt:{submission_id}` |
  | `ai_quiz_completed` (B7) | xp | per rule | AI quiz session | `xp:ai_quiz_completed:{session_id}` |
  | `challenge_reward` (B7) | xp or coins | per challenge | challenge | `reward:challenge:{challenge_id}` |
  | `freeze_granted` | freeze | +1 | streak milestone | `freeze:grant:{streak_started_on}:{milestone}` (for example `freeze:grant:2026-11-20:7`) |
  | `freeze_consumed` | freeze | −1 | missed Dhaka day | `freeze:consume:{dhaka_date}` |
  | `badge_awarded` (B7) | badge | 0 | badge code | `badge:{badge_code}` |
  | `coins_earned` (B9) | coins | per rule | same source as the XP row | `coins:{xp_idempotency_key}` |
  | `coins_spent` (B9) | coins | −cost | redemption | `coins:spend:{redemption_id}` |
  | `admin_reversal` | any | −original | transaction | `reverse:{transaction_id}` |
  | `freeze_returned` (B7) | freeze | +1 (reverses the consume row) | provisional day turned counted inside the 72 h window | `reverse:{transaction_id}` of the `freeze:consume:{dhaka_date}` row |

  Keys are unique per user, so the same `answer_id` used by two users cannot collide (C4).
- **Rule application inside the answer transaction** (savepoint): compute the candidate XP, then the 24 h repeat rule, then the daily cap (row lock on `daily_activity`, R3), then insert the ledger rows with `ON CONFLICT (user_id, idempotency_key) DO NOTHING`, then update `user_totals`. The rapid-guess flag does not reduce XP (C12).
- **Read/write pattern:**
  - About 2–3 rows per answer: about 40 rows/s at Stage 1, about 4,000–6,000 rows/s at Stage 4 (from the answer rates in [`17`](17_scalability_and_performance.md) §2.2).
  - Reads: history (`user_id`, recent first), and the leaderboard rebuild (stream by `recorded_at` per partition).
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `(user_id, id)`.
  - uq `(user_id, idempotency_key)`: no reward granted twice (gate §8.2).
  - `ix (user_id, recorded_at DESC)`: the history list.
  - BRIN `(recorded_at)`: board rebuild by period.
- **Scalability risks:** Second-largest table (about 0.4 TB in year 1). Never updated, so it suffers no bloat. It moves with `practice` at Stage 4.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; partition key; soft ref `identity.users` (no FK, §1.1) |
| track_enrolment_id | uuid | yes | — | soft ref `identity.track_enrolments` (no FK) |
| kind | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('xp','freeze','badge','coins') |
| amount | integer | no | — | CHECK (amount <> 0 OR kind = 'badge') |
| reason_code | text | no | — | CHECK IN (codes above) |
| source_type | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('answer','session','exam_attempt','question','dhaka_day','correction','challenge','streak','redemption','transaction') |
| source_id | text | no | — | uuid or date string |
| source_event_id | uuid | yes | — | Outbox event id when awarded by a consumer |
| idempotency_key | text | no | — | uq `(user_id, idempotency_key)`; CHECK (char_length <= 200) |
| rule_version | integer | no | — | — |
| effective_dhaka_date | date | no | — | Drives per-day rules |
| recorded_at | timestamptz | no | now() | Drives leaderboard periods (C12) |
| reverses_id | uuid | yes | — | — |

#### `rewards.user_totals`

- **Why:** A derived snapshot for fast reads: lifetime XP, level and daily goal settings (coins live in `coin_balances`, B9). It is updated in the same savepoint as the ledger and is rebuildable from the ledger.
- **Owner:** GAM.
- **Features:** GAM-01.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—1.
- **Read/write pattern:** Updated on every XP award (`UPDATE … SET xp_total = xp_total + :x, level = gam_level(xp_total + :x)`, with the level computed in Python and passed in). Read on every summary.
- **Level formula:** level *n* needs 50 × *n* × (*n* − 1) XP. Level is computed in the service as `floor((1 + sqrt(1 + xp/12.5)) / 2)` and verified by a table test.
- **Daily goal change:** writes `pending_goal_xp` and `pending_goal_from` (the next Dhaka date). The first read or award on or after that date promotes it.
- **Indexes:** PK `user_id`.
- **Scalability risks:** One row per user, updated per answer. Rows are narrow, `fillfactor=80` for HOT updates. There is no global hot row.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK; FK `identity.users(id)` |
| xp_total | bigint | no | 0 | CHECK (xp_total >= 0) |
| level | smallint | no | 1 | CHECK (level >= 1) |
| daily_goal_xp | smallint | no | 50 | CHECK (daily_goal_xp IN (20,50,100,150)) |
| pending_goal_xp | smallint | yes | — | CHECK (pending_goal_xp IN (20,50,100,150)) |
| pending_goal_from | date | yes | — | CHECK ((pending_goal_xp IS NULL) = (pending_goal_from IS NULL)) |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

#### `rewards.daily_activity`

- **Why:** Per-user, per-Dhaka-day rule state: answer count (streak threshold 5), exams submitted (a streak day also counts with 1 exam), XP from answers (the 300 cap), total XP and the daily goal. All are keyed by **effective** Dhaka date (C12).
- **Owner:** GAM.
- **Features:** GAM-01, GAM-02.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—N.
- **The cap (R3, R-19).** PostgreSQL's `RETURNING` cannot return pre-update values, so the cap is a row lock plus arithmetic in the service, inside the savepoint:
  ```
  INSERT INTO rewards.daily_activity (user_id, dhaka_date, goal_xp, rule_version)
       VALUES (:u, :d, :goal, :rv) ON CONFLICT (user_id, dhaka_date) DO NOTHING;
  SELECT xp_from_answers FROM rewards.daily_activity
   WHERE user_id = :u AND dhaka_date = :d FOR UPDATE;          -- old value, row locked
  -- service: awarded = max(0, min(:candidate, :cap - old))
  UPDATE rewards.daily_activity
     SET xp_from_answers = xp_from_answers + :awarded,
         answers_count   = answers_count + 1
   WHERE user_id = :u AND dhaka_date = :d;
  ```
  The ledger rows record awarded amounts. When the cap cuts an answer's XP to 0, the `answer_attempted` row is skipped, but `answers_count` still increments. The lock covers only one user's day, so there is no global hot row.
- **Daily goal:** when `xp_total` first reaches `goal_xp` (the goal in force on that date), set `goal_reached_at` and award `xp:daily_goal:{dhaka_date}`. That +10 counts toward `xp_total` but not toward `xp_from_answers`.
- **Read/write pattern:** One upsert per answer, at the same rate as answers. Read by the streak settlement job for the previous day.
- **Indexes:** PK `(user_id, dhaka_date)`; `ix (dhaka_date) WHERE answers_count >= 5 OR exams_submitted >= 1` for settlement scans (partial: only qualifying days).
- **Scalability risks:** About 1M rows a day at Stage 4. Rows older than 400 days are deleted, since `streak_days` keeps the settled outcome.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| dhaka_date | date | no | — | PK part |
| answers_count | integer | no | 0 | CHECK (answers_count >= 0) |
| exams_submitted | smallint | no | 0 | — |
| xp_from_answers | integer | no | 0 | CHECK (xp_from_answers >= 0) — the cap value comes from the rule version, not a constraint (see Proposed changes) |
| xp_total | integer | no | 0 | — |
| goal_xp | smallint | no | — | Goal in force on that date |
| goal_reached_at | timestamptz | yes | — | — |
| rule_version | integer | no | — | Rule version that set the cap for this day |

#### `rewards.streak_state`

- **Why:** The current streak, longest streak, freezes held (at most 2) and the settlement cursor.
- **Owner:** GAM.
- **Features:** GAM-02.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—1.
- **Counting and settlement rules (R-06):**
  - A day counts when `daily_activity` for that effective date has `answers_count >= 5` or `exams_submitted >= 1`. The qualifying answer writes `streak_days(D, 'counted')` if `D > last_settled_date`.
  - **Lazy settlement.** Before counting an answer for day D, the service locks `streak_state` (`FOR UPDATE`) and settles, in date order, every unsettled day d with `last_settled_date < d < D`. So an answer at 00:30 after a missed day settles that day with the freeze the user still holds, before today is counted.
  - **One settlement function** (`settle_through`) applies the rule per day, and it is the only place the length changes:
    - a counted day: `current_length + 1`;
    - a day without activity and with `freezes_held > 0`: `streak_days(d, 'frozen')`, ledger row `freeze:consume:{d}`, `freezes_held − 1`, length unchanged;
    - otherwise: `streak_days(d, 'missed')`, `current_length = 0`;
    - a counted day after a reset therefore becomes 1.

    Then `last_settled_date = d`, and `longest = GREATEST(longest, current_length)`.
  - **Finality.** At P0 (6 h window) days at or before `last_settled_date` are final (C12): a late answer still earns XP but leaves the streak unchanged. From B7, users with an active offline pack get a **provisional** settlement at 06:00 and a **final** one when the 72 h window closes. A late valid answer inside the window turns a provisional `missed` or `frozen` day into `counted` and returns a consumed freeze through a reversing ledger row. This is supervisor decision **S-9**. `streak_days` gains `is_provisional boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false` in B7.
- **Settlement job (`gam.streak.settle`, daily at 06:00 Dhaka):** covers only users with **no activity since their last settlement** (`last_settled_date < yesterday`), because active users were settled lazily. It runs in batches of 1,000 users, with `dedupe_key = settle:{range}:{date}`, applying `settle_through(user, today)` under the same lock.
- **Freeze grants:** each time a settled `current_length` reaches a multiple of 7, grant the key `freeze:grant:{streak_started_on}:{milestone}` if `freezes_held < 2`, using `LEAST(2, freezes_held + 1)` (R6).
- **Read/write pattern:**
  - Updated on the first answer per day (lazy settlement plus count): about 1 per active user per day.
  - The 06:00 job touches only inactive users with an open streak, which the partial index finds.
- **Indexes:** PK `user_id`; partial `ix (last_counted_date) WHERE current_length > 0`: the settlement scan.
- **Scalability risks:** Settlement at Stage 4 covers about 1M rows each morning, in batches over about 10 min, and must finish before 07:00 reminders. Measured in Build 9.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK; FK `identity.users(id)` |
| current_length | integer | no | 0 | CHECK (current_length >= 0) |
| longest | integer | no | 0 | CHECK (longest >= current_length) |
| last_counted_date | date | yes | — | — |
| last_settled_date | date | yes | — | — |
| streak_started_on | date | yes | — | First counted day of the current streak; part of the freeze-grant key |
| freezes_held | smallint | no | 0 | CHECK (freezes_held BETWEEN 0 AND 2) |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

#### `rewards.streak_days`

- **Why:** The settled outcome per day (counted, frozen, missed). It is the audit trail for streak disputes and the source for the streak calendar UI.
- **Owner:** GAM.
- **Features:** GAM-02.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—N.
- **Read/write pattern:** `counted` rows are inserted by the answer transaction (`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`). `frozen` and `missed` rows are inserted by settlement (lazy in the answer path, or by the 06:00 job for inactive users). From B7, provisional rows of offline-pack users may be updated once, from `missed` or `frozen` to `counted`, before final settlement (S-9). Read as a month range per user.
- **Indexes:** PK `(user_id, dhaka_date)`.
- **Scalability risks:** About 1M rows a day at Stage 4. Kept forever, at narrow rows (about 40 B).
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK part |
| dhaka_date | date | no | — | PK part |
| status | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('counted','frozen','missed') |
| settled_at | timestamptz | yes | — | NULL until the day is settled (lazily or by the 06:00 job) |
| is_provisional | boolean | no | false | Added in B7 for offline-pack users; final settlement at the end of the 72 h window clears it (S-9) |

#### `rewards.friend_codes`

- **Why:** A code or QR payload for creating the P0 mutual friend link: no search, no profiles and no messaging (v2 §5.4).
- **Owner:** GAM.
- **Features:** GAM-03.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—1.
- **Read/write pattern:** Created lazily. Rotated on request.
- **Indexes:** PK `user_id`; uq `code`.
- **Scalability risks:** Codes are 8 characters from a 32-symbol alphabet, which makes guessing hard. Redemption is rate-limited (T-write) and failures are counted.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| code | text | no | — | uq; CHECK (code ~ '^[23456789A-HJ-NP-Z]{8}$') |
| rotated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

#### `rewards.friend_links`

- **Why:** A mutual friend link, stored once per pair. It drives the friends leaderboard.
- **Owner:** GAM.
- **Features:** GAM-03, GAM-09 (extends to follows in Build 9).
- **Relations:** `identity.users` N—N with itself.
- **Read/write pattern:** Rare writes. The friends board reads friend ids (at most 200) and then `ZMSCORE` on the weekly board key.
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `(user_a_id, user_b_id)` with `CHECK (user_a_id < user_b_id)`: one row per pair.
  - `ix (user_b_id) WHERE removed_at IS NULL`: reverse lookup.
- **Scalability risks:** At most 200 friends per user (service rule).
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_a_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `identity.users(id)` |
| user_b_id | uuid | no | — | PK part; FK `identity.users(id)`; CHECK (user_a_id < user_b_id) |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |
| removed_at | timestamptz | yes | — | — |

#### `rewards.board_flags`

- **Why:** Anti-abuse. Rapid-guess flags (attempt flag bit 1) are aggregated nightly. Accounts with repeated flags are hidden from public boards pending review, and their learning records are untouched (v2 §5.1).
- **Owner:** GAM.
- **Features:** GAM-01, GAM-03.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—1.
- **Read/write pattern:** A nightly upsert from the attempt flags of the previous day, through the GAM consumer of `answer.recorded`, which counts flagged answers. Read when boards are rendered: hidden users are removed with `ZREM` and skipped on rebuild.
- **Indexes:** PK `user_id`; `ix (hidden_from_boards) WHERE hidden_from_boards`.
- **Scalability risks:** None.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| user_id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| flag_count | integer | no | 0 | — |
| hidden_from_boards | boolean | no | false | — |
| reason | text | yes | — | — |
| reviewed_by | uuid | yes | — | FK `identity.users(id)` |
| reviewed_at | timestamptz | yes | — | — |
| updated_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

#### `rewards.leaderboard_snapshots`

- **Why:** Frozen top-100 lists of closed periods, so that "a closed board never changes" holds even after a Redis rebuild (C12).
- **Owner:** GAM.
- **Features:** GAM-03.
- **Relations:** None (keyed by board key).
- **Read/write pattern:** Written at the period rollover (Saturday 00:00 Dhaka for weekly, the 1st for monthly). Read for "last week's winners".
- **Indexes:** PK `(board_key, period_key)`.
- **Scalability risks:**
  - Board keys multiply as tracks × audiences × institutions × districts.
  - Snapshots are taken only for national and district boards (about 64 × tracks).
  - Institution and friends boards are not snapshotted; they show only the live period.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|

### 4.12 `commerce`

All amounts are integer poisha (`bigint`, CHECK ≥ 0). Provider adapters convert to and from decimal strings at the boundary, using the starter's `money.py`.

#### State machines

Transitions are allowed only as listed. Every transition happens in a service under the order row lock (`SELECT … FOR UPDATE`) and writes an outbox event. Any other transition raises `ConflictError("invalid_transition")`.

**Order (`orders.status`)**

| From | To | Trigger |
|---|---|---|
| — | `created` | `POST /v1/orders` (amount, coupon and payable computed) |
| `created` | `pending` | Provider payment created (bKash create, aggregator session, Play purchase pending) |
| `created`, `pending` | `paid` | Payment `captured` (in the same transaction) |
| `created`, `pending` | `failed` | Provider reports failure or cancellation |
| `created`, `pending` | `expired` | Sweeper at `expires_at` (24 h, PAY-05) after a final status query |
| `failed`, `expired` | `paid` | Reconciliation finds the payment completed at the provider ("paid there, not here": capture and grant) |
| `paid` | `partially_refunded` | Partial refund `completed` |
| `paid`, `partially_refunded` | `refunded` | Refunds total `payable_poisha` |

**Payment (`payments.status`)**

| From | To | Trigger |
|---|---|---|
| — | `initiated` | Provider create call returned `provider_payment_id` |
| `initiated` | `execute_requested` | Under the order lock, `execute_requested_at` is set and **committed before** the bKash Execute call (R-08). A crash after this point can never lead to a second Execute. |
| `execute_requested` | `executed` | bKash Execute returned success (one execute per payment id, R10) |
| `execute_requested` | `execute_unknown` | Execute timed out, failed at the network level, or the process crashed (sweeper finds `execute_requested` older than 60 s). **Never execute again.** |
| `execute_unknown` | `executed`, `failed` | Status query result |
| (any successful provider payment) | `duplicate_captured` | The order is already `paid` through a different payment. A reconciliation exception `duplicate_payment` is opened, and job `pay.refund_duplicate` starts. |
| `duplicate_captured` | `refunded` | Provider refund confirmed; credit note issued |
| `initiated` | `failed` | User cancelled, or the provider declined |
| `executed` | `captured` | Server-side verification: status query or validated IPN, with amount = `payable_poisha` and order match; in one transaction with entitlement, invoice and outbox |
| `initiated` (aggregator, Play) | `captured` | Verified IPN, validation API or purchase-token verification (no execute step) |
| `executed` | `failed` | Status query shows a reversal before capture |
| `captured` | `reversed` | Provider reversal or chargeback found by reconciliation (suspend entitlement, alert finance) |

**Refund (`refunds.status`)**

| From | To | Trigger |
|---|---|---|
| — | `requested` | Ops admin with `pay.refund` and a reason (audited) |
| `requested` | `submitted` | Provider refund API accepted |
| `requested` | `rejected` | Validation: amount above refundable remainder, or a live-exam fee after the exam start |
| `submitted` | `completed` | Provider confirms; entitlement shortened or revoked, credit note issued, outbox `entitlement.changed` |
| `submitted` | `failed` | Provider rejects; exception opened |

**Entitlement (`entitlements.status`)**

| From | To | Trigger |
|---|---|---|
| — | `active` | Grant: payment, coupon, admin, institution seat, trial, scholarship or referral |
| `active` | `expired` | `ends_at` passed (nightly job; access checks also compare `ends_at` directly, so the job is cosmetic) |
| `active` | `suspended` | Reconciliation reversal or fraud hold |
| `suspended` | `active` | Exception resolved in the user's favour |
| `active`, `suspended` | `revoked` | Full refund, or admin revoke |
| `active` | `active` (`ends_at` changed) | Partial refund shortens; admin extends. Each change records an `entitlement_events` row with before and after. |

#### `commerce.products`

- **Why:** The catalogue: track passes, exam series, single exams, AI credit packs, marking packs and institution licences. Validity is a number of days or "until the exam date" (PAY-01).
- **Owner:** PAY.
- **Features:** PAY-01, EXAM-04 (fee), EXAM-11, AI-05a (credits), AI-07 (marking quota, D2).
- **Relations:** 1—N `product_grants`, `entitlements`, `orders`, `price_points`; `curriculum.tracks` 0..1—N via `until_exam_track_id`.
- **Read/write pattern:** Staff writes. Cached for 5 min; the public catalogue is edge-cached.
- **Indexes:** PK `id`; uq `code`; `ix (status, kind)`.
- **Scalability risks:** None. `price_poisha` mirrors the current `price_points` row, updated in the same transaction.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| code | text | no | — | uq; CHECK (code ~ '^[a-z0-9_]{3,60}$') |
| kind | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('track_pass','exam_series','single_exam','ai_credits','marking_pack','institution_licence','bundle','mentor_credits','notes') |
| name_bn | text | no | — | — |
| name_en | text | no | — | — |
| price_poisha | bigint | no | — | CHECK (price_poisha >= 0) |
| validity_kind | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('days','until_exam_date','none') |
| validity_days | integer | yes | — | CHECK ((validity_kind='days') = (validity_days IS NOT NULL)) |
| until_exam_track_id | uuid | yes | — | FK `curriculum.tracks(id)`; CHECK ((validity_kind='until_exam_date') = (until_exam_track_id IS NOT NULL)) |
| status | text | no | 'draft' | CHECK IN ('draft','active','retired') |
| play_product_id | text | yes | — | uq partial |
| apple_product_id | text | yes | — | uq partial (Build 8) |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

#### `commerce.product_grants`

- **Why:** What a product unlocks: content scope (track, syllabus version, node, exam, series) or a feature with a quota.
- **Owner:** PAY.
- **Features:** PAY-01, PAY-02.
- **Relations:** `products` 1—N.
- **Read/write pattern:** Read during entitlement resolution and cached per product.
- **Indexes:** PK `id`; `ix (product_id)`; `ix (scope_type, scope_id)` to find which products unlock a piece of content (for the upgrade prompt).
- **Scalability risks:** None.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| product_id | uuid | no | — | FK `products(id)` CASCADE |
| scope_type | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('track','syllabus_version','node','exam','exam_series','feature') |
| scope_id | uuid | yes | — | CHECK ((scope_type='feature') = (scope_id IS NULL)) |
| feature_code | text | yes | — | CHECK IN ('ai_messages','ai_photos','live_exams','marking','questions') |
| quota | jsonb | yes | — | e.g. `{"per":"dhaka_day","limit":100}` or a credit pack `{"total":500}` |

#### `commerce.entitlements`

- **Why:** Access records. Access is the **union** of active entitlements plus free quotas, so overlapping grants and partial refunds behave correctly (v2 §7.2).
- **Owner:** PAY.
- **Features:** PAY-02, PAY-05, OPS-01 (manual grant), INST-02 (seats, Build 8), PAR-04 (beneficiary, Build 8), PAY-08.
- **Relations:** `identity.users` 1—N; `products` 1—N; 1—N `entitlement_events`; `source_ref` points to an order, coupon, seat, sponsorship or admin action.
- **Read/write pattern:**
  - Read on every gated request: the per-user resolved set is cached in process for 30 s and invalidated on `entitlement.changed`, so about 1 DB read per user per 30 s.
  - Writes are rare.
- **Indexes:**
  - PK `id`.
  - `ix_entitlements_user_active (user_id, status, ends_at)`: the resolution query.
  - uq partial `(user_id, product_id) WHERE source='trial'`: one trial per product.
  - `ix (source, source_ref)`: the refund lookup.
- **Scalability risks:** None material. At Stage 4 the cache keeps reads around 3k/s across all users.
- **Build:** 3. Build 8 adds `beneficiary_user_id` (expand).

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| user_id | uuid | no | — | FK `identity.users(id)`; the holder (the payer for family plans) |
| beneficiary_user_id | uuid | yes | — | Build 8; FK `identity.users(id)`; access is checked against `COALESCE(beneficiary_user_id, user_id)` |
| product_id | uuid | no | — | FK `products(id)` |
| source | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('payment','coupon','admin','institution_seat','trial','scholarship','referral','reward_store','family_plan') |
| source_ref | text | yes | — | — |
| starts_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |
| ends_at | timestamptz | yes | — | NULL = no expiry; CHECK (ends_at IS NULL OR ends_at > starts_at) |
| status | text | no | 'active' | CHECK IN ('active','suspended','revoked','expired') |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

#### `commerce.entitlement_events`

- **Why:** The full history of each entitlement (granted, extended, shortened, suspended, revoked, expired), with actor and reason (v2 §3.6 source of truth).
- **Owner:** PAY.
- **Features:** PAY-02, PAY-05, OPS-04 (admin changes are also audited).
- **Relations:** `entitlements` 1—N.
- **Read/write pattern:** Append-only; one row per change.
- **Indexes:** PK `id`; `ix (entitlement_id, created_at)`.
- **Scalability risks:** None.
- **Build:** 3.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| entitlement_id | uuid | no | — | FK `entitlements(id)` |
| kind | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('granted','extended','shortened','suspended','reactivated','revoked','expired') |
| reason | text | no | — | — |
| actor_id | uuid | yes | — | NULL for system |
| before | jsonb | yes | — | — |
| after | jsonb | no | — | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | no | now() | — |

#### `commerce.quota_policies`

- **Why:** Free and paid quotas per feature and period: questions per day, AI messages per day, photo solves per day, live exams per week, marking per month (PAY-02, AI-05a). Values are data, so D2 can change them without a deploy.
- **Owner:** PAY.
- **Features:** PAY-02, AI-05a, AI-15.
- **Relations:** None (keyed by plan code).
- **Read/write pattern:** Read on process start and refreshed every 60 s. Usage counters live in Redis (`quota:*`, §3.17).
- **Indexes:** PK `id`; uq `(plan_code, feature, effective_from)`.
- **Scalability risks:** None.
- **Build:** 3. Seed v1: free 10 messages and 2 photos a day; paid 100 and 20 (v2 §4.6).

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | — | PK |
| plan_code | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('free','paid','institution') |
| feature | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('questions','ai_messages','ai_photos','live_exams','marking','ai_quiz','voice_minutes','spoken_english_minutes','viva_sessions') |
| limit_value | integer | no | — | CHECK (limit_value >= 0) |
| period | text | no | — | CHECK IN ('dhaka_day','dhaka_week','dhaka_month') |
| effective_from | timestamptz | no | — | — |

- **Why:** The price history per product, so invoices and analytics can show the price that applied at the time.
- **Owner:** PAY.

- **Relations:** `products` 1—N.
- **Read/write pattern:** Rare writes.
- **Indexes:** PK `id`; uq `(product_id, effective_from)`.

- **Build:** 6.

| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraints |

| id | uuid | no | — | PK |

| price_poisha | bigint | no | — | CHECK (price_poisha >= 0) |
